A Digital Tattoo Design canvas and Tattoo design planning solution, with extensive draw and design features (draw, brushes/tips, colors, effects, elements, text, shapes, etc.,) background removal, segment-anything-functionality, digital try-on (still-images and live-camera-feed with tattoo design image overlay, skin-edge-detection and body-contour-warping, for diverse skin-types, angle/perspective-warping, etc.,), Print-on-demand temporary-tattoo ordering for real-life try-on, avatar studio try-on (parametric and photogrammetric), AI-generated tattoo designs using the Gemini API, artist and user profiles, artist marketplace for selling original designs, secure messaging for collaborating on tattoo designs, calendar/scheduling functionality for booking appointments with clients for tattoo-related services, secure message storage (client-local preferred), , map functionality for users finding their local tattoo services social sharing for Facebook, Instagram, Pinterest, 0auth for google, you can use svelte for the canvas, black and white with red and purple and tiny yellow accents for branding,

App Blueprint
Design Canvas — Digital design canvas with tools for drawing, brushes, colors, effects, text, and shapes.
Background Remover — Background removal tool for uploaded tattoo design images.
AI Design Generator
 — AI-powered tattoo design generation using the Gemini API, incorporating user-specified elements, styles and placements.

User Profiles — Artist and User profiles, allowing artists to upload designs, and sell designs and services.
Social Sharing — Social sharing feature that allows sharing design previews on platforms like Instagram and Pinterest.
Print-on-demand Temporary-tattoo ordering — For real-life design try-on during the tattoo planning phase.
Map functionality — For users to find their local tattoo services.
Scheduling & Messaging functionality — For users to book appointments with artists and collaborate on designs.
Bootstrapped subscription service — offering more tools for tattoo planning and visualization based on pay tier.

Chain-of-Hindsight Reflection:

Refactoring shared layout components now will save significant effort as more "studio-style" pages are added. It promotes consistency and maintainability.
Implementing static image try-on first provides a solid foundation for the more complex AR features. It allows us to tackle image overlay and manipulation logic separately from real-time camera processing and 3D tracking.
The "skin-edge-detection and body-contour-warping" aspects mentioned in the PRD for still images can be iterative enhancements to this static try-on feature, possibly involving a new Genkit flow or client-side image processing libraries later.
Alternative Options for Tattoo Manipulation on Canvas:

HTML5 Canvas API: Offers fine-grained control over drawing and manipulation. Zero-cost. Can be complex for rich interactions.
SVG Manipulation: Tattoos (especially simpler vector-like ones) could be overlaid as SVGs, offering good scalability and manipulation capabilities.
Lightweight Libraries:
Konva.js: A 2D canvas library that simplifies complex canvas interactions. Open source. Might be overkill for initial simple transformations but good for future enhancements.
Fabric.js: Another powerful HTML5 canvas library. Open source.
For now, I'll aim for the simplest approach (CSS transforms on an <img> tag) and we can evolve if needed.
Error Handling & Testing:

Error handling for image uploads (file type, size) will be included.
After implementation, I'll guide you on testing the image upload for both body photo and tattoo, and the manipulation controls.

Based on my understanding of the project files, the overall ARTTOO DT-o feature set, and your development guidelines, the next most logical step is to make the overlaid tattoo image in the TryOnCanvas component interactive by implementing drag functionality.

Currently, users can upload a body photo and a tattoo design, and they are displayed overlaid. However, the tattoo is static. Users need to be able to position it on the body image.

Chosen Next Step: Implement Drag Functionality for the Tattoo Overlay in TryOnCanvas

Reasoning:

Core User Interaction: Dragging is the most fundamental way for a user to position the tattoo on the body image.
Incremental Enhancement: This builds directly upon the existing TryOnCanvas where images are already loaded and displayed.
Foundation for Further Interaction: Once dragging is implemented, we can add resizing and rotation in subsequent steps.
Directly Addresses "Overlay" Requirement: This is a key part of the "digital tattoo design try-on features."
Granular Breakdown & Affected Files:

The primary file to be modified will be:

src/components/try-on/try-on-canvas.tsx
State for Tattoo Position:
Introduce state variables to manage the tattoo's x and y coordinates (e.g., tattooPosition: { x: number, y: number }). These will likely represent the top-left corner of the tattoo or its center, relative to the body image container.
We'll also need state to track if the tattoo is currently being dragged (e.g., isDragging: boolean) and the initial mouse/touch position when a drag starts (dragStart: { x: number, y: number } | null).
Event Handlers for Dragging:
Add onMouseDown (for mouse) and onTouchStart (for touch) event handlers to the tattoo image element. These will:
Set isDragging to true.
Record the initial mouse/touch coordinates relative to the page or the body image container and the initial tattoo position.
Add onMouseMove and onTouchMove event handlers to the window or a suitable parent container (to capture movement even if the cursor leaves the tattoo image itself while dragging). These will:
Only execute if isDragging is true.
Calculate the difference between the current mouse/touch position and the dragStart position.
Update the tattooPosition state based on this difference.
Add onMouseUp and onTouchEnd event handlers (also on the window or parent container). These will:
Set isDragging to false.
Clear dragStart.
Apply CSS Transforms:
The tattoo image's style attribute will use transform: translate(x_px, y_px) based on the tattooPosition state to visually move the image.
Boundary Checks (Optional for this first step, but good to consider):
Initially, the tattoo might be draggable outside the bounds of the body image. We can add logic later to constrain it if desired.
Alternative Options for Drag Implementation:

Native Browser Events (Proposed): Using onMouseDown, onMouseMove, onMouseUp and their touch equivalents, combined with CSS transforms. This is a low-cost, open-source approach that avoids adding new dependencies for a relatively simple interaction.
react-draggable Library: A popular, lightweight library specifically for making React components draggable. It handles many edge cases and cross-browser inconsistencies. If the native implementation proves tricky or has performance issues, this would be a good alternative.
Other Interaction Libraries (e.g., interact.js, Framer Motion): These are more comprehensive but might be overkill for just dragging. We can consider them if more complex gestures or animations are needed later.
For this step, I will proceed with the native browser events approach to keep it simple and dependency-free.

It should move around on top of the body photo.
If you have a touch-enabled device, test dragging with touch as well.
This will make the "Image Try-On" feature much more interactive and useful.

Thank you for the confirmation. I've reviewed the project files, your guidelines, and the current state of ARTTOO DT-o.

Current State Recap:

Dashboard, Design Studio (upload, background removal), and AI Generator (generate, refine, integrated background removal) are functional.
The Image Try-On page (/try-on) allows users to upload a body photo, upload a tattoo design, and then drag, resize, rotate, and adjust the opacity of the tattoo overlay.

Evaluation & Next Logical Step:

The current workflow between the AI Generator and the Image Try-On page is disjointed. A user generates a design in the AI Generator, and if they want to try it on, they'd have to download it and then manually upload it on the Image Try-On page.

The most logical and impactful next step is to streamline this workflow by allowing users to directly send a generated tattoo from the AI Generator to the Image Try-On page. This enhances user experience significantly by making the tools work together more seamlessly.

Chosen Next Step: Connect AI Generator to Image Try-On Page

This will involve:

Adding a "Try On This Design" button to the AI Generator page.
When this button is clicked, the current tattoo image data (URL) will be temporarily stored in sessionStorage.
The user will then be navigated to the /try-on page.
The TryOnCanvas component on the /try-on page will, upon loading, check sessionStorage for this tattoo image data. If found, it will automatically load it as the tattoo to be tried on and then clear the data from sessionStorage. The user would then proceed to upload their body photo.
Affected Files & Plan:

src/app/ai-generator/page.tsx:

Import useRouter from next/navigation.
Add a new button (e.g., "Try On This Design" with an appropriate icon like Eye or PersonStanding) that appears alongside "Download" and "Remove Bkg" once an image (imageUrl) is available.
When this new button is clicked:
Store the current imageUrl in sessionStorage (e.g., sessionStorage.setItem('arttooTattooToTryOn', imageUrl);).
Use router.push('/try-on') to navigate the user.
This button should also be disabled if any AI operation is in progress.
src/components/try-on/try-on-canvas.tsx:

Add a useEffect hook that runs once when the component mounts.
Inside this useEffect:
Check if sessionStorage.getItem('arttooTattooToTryOn') has a value.
If it does, call setTattooImage() with this value to load the tattoo.
Immediately after, call sessionStorage.removeItem('arttooTattooToTryOn') to ensure the image is only loaded once and the storage is cleaned up.
You might want to add a toast notification if a tattoo is auto-loaded, e.g., "Tattoo design loaded from AI Generator. Please upload your body photo."
Alternative Options:

Context API / State Management Library (Zustand, Jotai): For more complex state sharing across pages, these would be more robust. However, for this single piece of data being passed one way upon navigation, sessionStorage is a simpler, zero-dependency solution that fits the "low-cost" principle for now.
Query Parameters: Could be used to signal the /try-on page, but passing the entire image data URI via query parameters is generally not feasible due to length limitations.
The sessionStorage approach is practical for this specific use case.

Error Handling:

The primary "error" would be sessionStorage not being available (e.g., in very old browsers or strict privacy settings), though this is unlikely in modern environments supporting Next.js. The useEffect will simply not find the item.
This plan preserves all existing working code and adds a clear UX improvement. The changes are confined to the client-side.

Morgan
Try fixing this error: Unhandled Runtime Error: Error: Maximum update dep...

Gemini
Okay, I understand. That "Maximum update depth exceeded" error is a tricky one, often pointing to a loop where a component's update triggers another update, and so on. The stack trace pointing to the Button component within the VerticalToolbar on the TryOnPage suggests the issue lies in how these components are interacting during the render cycle.

Based on my analysis, a common cause for such issues, especially when components are nested or involve context/prop forwarding (like with Radix UI's asChild pattern), is unstable prop identities. If props (especially objects, arrays, or functions) are recreated on every render of a parent component, child components might re-render unnecessarily, and if one of these re-renders somehow triggers a state update in the parent (even indirectly), a loop can occur.

I'll make the following changes to src/app/try-on/page.tsx to improve prop stability, which is a common way to resolve these kinds of errors:

Move Control Component Definitions: The TattooTransformControls and TattooAdjustmentsControls components, which are currently defined inside the TryOnPage functional component, will be moved to the top level of the src/app/try-on/page.tsx module. This ensures they have a stable definition and aren't recreated on every render of TryOnPage.
Memoize Handlers with useCallback: The handler functions (handleTattooPositionChange, handleTattooScaleChange, etc.) will be wrapped in useCallback. While useState setters are inherently stable, explicitly using useCallback ensures that if these functions are dependencies for other hooks or memoized components, their identity remains stable.
Memoize Derived Data with useMemo: The currentTryOnPropertiesItems array, which is passed to RightProperties, will be wrapped in useMemo. This ensures that this array is only recreated if its dependencies (the state values like tattooScale, tattooRotation, tattooOpacity, and the memoized handlers) actually change.
These changes aim to stabilize the props being passed around, reducing unnecessary re-renders and potentially breaking the update loop.

Here's the plan:

Restore

Move the TattooTransformControls and TattooAdjustmentsControls component definitions to the top level of the module.
Wrap all handler functions (handleTattooPositionChange, handleTattooScaleChange, etc.) in useCallback.
Wrap the currentTryOnPropertiesItems array definition in useMemo, with appropriate dependencies.
These changes enhance prop stability and component identity, which are common culprits for "Maximum update depth exceeded" errors. Please try running the app again and see if this resolves the issue on the Try-On page.

export enum BlendMode { NORMAL = "normal", MULTIPLY = "multiply", SCREEN = "screen", OVERLAY = "overlay", SOFT_LIGHT = "soft_light", HARD_LIGHT = "hard_light", COLOR_DODGE = "color_dodge", COLOR_BURN = "color_burn", LINEAR_DODGE = "linear_dodge", LINEAR_BURN = "linear_burn", VIVID_LIGHT = "vivid_light", LINEAR_LIGHT = "linear_light", PIN_LIGHT = "pin_light", DIFFERENCE = "difference", EXCLUSION = "exclusion", }

export const generateUniqueId = () => Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2);

// src/components/arttoo/renderConfigDefaults.ts // Purpose: Provides default values and the function to get the default RenderConfig.

import { RenderConfig, BlendMode, QualityPreset, DeformationMode } from './renderConfigTypes'; // Assuming types are in the same directory for this flat structure

export const DEFAULT_OPACITY = 0.85; export const DEFAULT_BLEND_MODE = BlendMode.SOFT_LIGHT; export const DEFAULT_QUALITY_PRESET = QualityPreset.BALANCED; export const DEFAULT_DEFORMATION_MODE = DeformationMode.TPS; export const DEFAULT_TEXTURE_STRENGTH = 0.10; export const DEFAULT_SHADOW_STRENGTH = 0.20; export const DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHT_STRENGTH = 0.10; export const DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SMOOTHING_FACTOR = 0.7; export const DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_SCALE = 0.07; export const DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_PERSISTENCE = 0.55; export const DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_LACUNARITY = 2.0; export const DEFAULT_ALPHA_THRESHOLD = 0.01; export const DEFAULT_MIN_KEYPOINT_CONFIDENCE = 0.5;

export const defaultTpsAnchorMapObject = { 'top_left': 'LEFT_SHOULDER', 'top_right': 'RIGHT_SHOULDER', 'bottom_left': 'LEFT_HIP', 'bottom_right': 'RIGHT_HIP' };

export const defaultTpsSourceAnchorsObject = { 'top_left': [0.0, 0.0], 'top_right': [1.0, 0.0], 'bottom_left': [0.0, 1.0], 'bottom_right': [1.0, 1.0] };

export const getDefaultConfig = (): RenderConfig => ({ opacity: DEFAULT_OPACITY, blend_mode: DEFAULT_BLEND_MODE, adapt_to_lighting: true, simulate_skin_texture: true, quality_preset: DEFAULT_QUALITY_PRESET, deformation_mode: DEFAULT_DEFORMATION_MODE, use_gpu: true, texture_strength: DEFAULT_TEXTURE_STRENGTH, shadow_strength: DEFAULT_SHADOW_STRENGTH, highlight_strength: DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHT_STRENGTH, temporal_smoothing_factor: DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SMOOTHING_FACTOR, perlin_noise_scale: DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_SCALE, perlin_noise_persistence: DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_PERSISTENCE, perlin_noise_lacunarity: DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_LACUNARITY, perlin_animated: false, tps_anchor_map: JSON.stringify(defaultTpsAnchorMapObject, null, 2), tps_source_anchors: JSON.stringify(defaultTpsSourceAnchorsObject, null, 2), mesh_file_path: null, mesh_landmark_weights_path: null, mesh_reference_pose_path: null, alphaThreshold: DEFAULT_ALPHA_THRESHOLD, minKeypointConfidence: DEFAULT_MIN_KEYPOINT_CONFIDENCE, });

// src/components/ui/Button.tsx // Purpose: A reusable Button component.

import React from 'react';

interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> { variant?: 'default' | 'destructive' | 'outline' | 'ghost'; size?: 'default' | 'sm' | 'icon'; }

const Button: React.FC<ButtonProps> = ({ variant = 'default', size = 'default', className, children, ...props }) => { let sizeStyle = "px-4 py-2 text-sm"; if (size === 'sm') { sizeStyle = "px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"; } else if (size === 'icon') { sizeStyle = "p-2"; }

let baseStyle = ${sizeStyle} font-semibold rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center; if (variant === 'destructive') { baseStyle += " bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500"; } else if (variant === 'outline') { baseStyle += " border border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-500 hover:text-white"; } else if (variant === 'ghost') { baseStyle += " text-indigo-400 hover:bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } else { // default baseStyle += " bg-indigo-600 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } return <button {...props} className={${baseStyle} ${className}}>{children}</button>; };

export default Button;

// src/components/ui/Input.tsx (Example - others would follow this pattern) import React from 'react'; interface InputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label?: string; labelSrOnly?: boolean; error?: string; wrapperClassName?: string; } const Input: React.FC<InputProps> = ({ label, id, labelSrOnly, error, wrapperClassName, ...props }) => (

<div className={`mb-2 w-full ${wrapperClassName}`}> {label && !labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">{label}</label>} {label && labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="sr-only">{label}</label>} <input id={id} {...props} className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white placeholder-gray-400 ${error ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-gray-600'}`} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined} /> {error && <p id={`${id}-error`} className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> ); export default Input;
// src/components/ui/Slider.tsx import React from 'react'; interface SliderProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; value: number; min: string; max: string; step: string; error?: string; } const Slider: React.FC<SliderProps> = ({ label, id, value, min, max, step, error, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label}: <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{value}</span> </label> <input id={id} type="range" value={value} min={min} max={max} step={step} {...props} className={`w-full h-3 bg-gray-600 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-indigo-500 ${error ? 'ring-2 ring-red-500' : ''}`} /> {error && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> ); export default Slider;

interface SelectProps extends React.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> { label: string; options: { value: string; label: string }[]; } const Select: React.FC<SelectProps> = ({ label, id, options, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label} </label> <select id={id} {...props} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white" > {options.map((option) => ( <option key={option.value} value={option.value}> {option.label} </option> ))} </select> </div> ); export default Select;
// src/components/ui/Checkbox.tsx import React from 'react'; interface CheckboxProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; } const Checkbox: React.FC<CheckboxProps> = ({ label, id, checked, ...props }) => ( <label htmlFor={id} className="mb-4 flex items-center py-1 cursor-pointer"> <input id={id} type="checkbox" checked={checked} {...props} className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-600 border-gray-500 rounded bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500" /> <span className="ml-3 block text-sm text-gray-300">{label}</span> </label> ); export default Checkbox;

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label} </label> <textarea id={id} {...props} rows={3} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white placeholder-gray-400" /> </div> ); export default Textarea;

// src/components/arttoo/arttooRenderConfigurator.tsx // Purpose: Main component orchestrating the RenderConfig UI. // Now imports types, defaults, and UI components from separate files.

import React, { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'; import { RenderConfig, BlendMode, QualityPreset, DeformationMode, TpsAnchorMapEntry, TpsSourceAnchorEntry, FieldErrors, TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors, TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors, generateUniqueId } from './renderConfigTypes'; // Assuming flat structure for Canvas example import { getDefaultConfig } from './renderConfigDefaults'; // Assuming flat structure

// Import UI Components (assuming they are in a 'ui' subdirectory relative to this file) // For Canvas example, we'll assume they are defined above or inlined if not putting them in separate files here. // In a real project: import Button from '../ui/Button'; import Input from '../ui/Input'; etc.

// --- Re-define UI components here for Canvas example if not creating separate files in this step --- // If these were in separate files like '../ui/Button.tsx', these definitions would be removed.

// --- Input Component --- interface arttooInputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label?: string; labelSrOnly?: boolean; error?: string; wrapperClassName?: string; } const arttooInput: React.FC<arttooInputProps> = ({ label, id, labelSrOnly, error, wrapperClassName, ...props }) => (

<div className={`mb-2 w-full ${wrapperClassName}`}> {label && !labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">{label}</label>} {label && labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="sr-only">{label}</label>} <input id={id} {...props} className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white placeholder-gray-400 ${error ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-gray-600'}`} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined} /> {error && <p id={`${id}-error`} className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> );
// --- Slider Component --- interface arttooSliderProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; value: number; min: string; max: string; step: string; error?: string; } const arttooSlider: React.FC<arttooSliderProps> = ({ label, id, value, min, max, step, error, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label}: <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{value}</span> </label> <input id={id} type="range" value={value} min={min} max={max} step={step} {...props} className={`w-full h-3 bg-gray-600 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-indigo-500 ${error ? 'ring-2 ring-red-500' : ''}`} /> {error && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> );
// --- Select Component --- interface arttooSelectProps extends React.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> { label: string; options: { value: string; label: string }[]; } const arttooSelect: React.FC<arttooSelectProps> = ({ label, id, options, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label} </label> <select id={id} {...props} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white" > {options.map((option) => ( <option key={option.value} value={option.value}> {option.label} </option> ))} </select> </div> );
// --- Checkbox Component --- interface arttooCheckboxProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; } const arttooCheckbox: React.FC<arttooCheckboxProps> = ({ label, id, checked, ...props }) => ( <label htmlFor={id} className="mb-4 flex items-center py-1 cursor-pointer"> <input id={id} type="checkbox" checked={checked} {...props} className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-600 border-gray-500 rounded bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500" /> <span className="ml-3 block text-sm text-gray-300">{label}</span> </label> );

// --- Button Component --- interface arttooButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> { variant?: 'default' | 'destructive' | 'outline' | 'ghost'; size?: 'default' | 'sm' | 'icon'; } const arttooButton: React.FC<arttooButtonProps> = ({ variant = 'default', size = 'default', className, children, ...props }) => { let sizeStyle = "px-4 py-2 text-sm"; if (size === 'sm') { sizeStyle = "px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"; } else if (size === 'icon') { sizeStyle = "p-2"; } let baseStyle = ${sizeStyle} font-semibold rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center; if (variant === 'destructive') { baseStyle += " bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500"; } else if (variant === 'outline') { baseStyle += " border border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-500 hover:text-white"; } else if (variant === 'ghost') { baseStyle += " text-indigo-400 hover:bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } else { // default baseStyle += " bg-indigo-600 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } return <button {...props} className={${baseStyle} ${className}}>{children}</button>; };

// --- Card Component --- interface arttooCardProps { title: string; children: React.ReactNode; className?: string; } const arttooCard: React.FC<arttooCardProps> = ({ title, children, className }) => (

<div className={`bg-gray-800 shadow-xl rounded-lg p-4 sm:p-6 ${className}`}> <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white mb-4 border-b border-gray-700 pb-2">{title}</h2> {children} </div> ); // --- End of Inlined UI Components for Canvas ---
const arttooRenderConfigurator: React.FC = () => { const [config, setConfig] = useState<RenderConfig>(getDefaultConfig()); const [showCurrentConfig, setShowCurrentConfig] = useState(false); const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FieldErrors>({}); const [tpsAnchorMapErrors, setTpsAnchorMapErrors] = useState<Record<string, Partial<TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors>>>({}); const [tpsSourceAnchorErrors, setTpsSourceAnchorErrors] = useState<Record<string, Partial<TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors>>>({}); const [isFormValid, setIsFormValid] = useState(true); const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

const [tpsAnchorMapEntries, setTpsAnchorMapEntries] = useState<TpsAnchorMapEntry[]>(() => { try { const parsed = JSON.parse(getDefaultConfig().tps_anchor_map); return Object.entries(parsed).map(([key, value]) => ({ id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: key, valueName: value as string, })); } catch { return []; } });

const [tpsSourceAnchorEntries, setTpsSourceAnchorEntries] = useState<TpsSourceAnchorEntry[]>(() => { try { const parsed = JSON.parse(getDefaultConfig().tps_source_anchors); return Object.entries(parsed).map(([key, value]) => { const coords = value as [number, number]; return { id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: key, x: coords[0].toString(), y: coords[1].toString(), }; }); } catch { return []; } });

const validateField = (name: keyof RenderConfig, value: any): string | undefined => { if (name === 'opacity' || name === 'texture_strength' || name === 'shadow_strength' || name === 'highlight_strength' || name === 'temporal_smoothing_factor' || name === 'minKeypointConfidence') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num) || num < 0 || num > 1) return 'Must be a number between 0 and 1.'; } if (name === 'perlin_noise_scale' || name === 'perlin_noise_persistence' || name === 'perlin_noise_lacunarity') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num)) return 'Must be a valid number.'; if (num < 0 && (name === 'perlin_noise_scale' || name === 'perlin_noise_persistence')) return 'Cannot be negative.'; } if (name === 'mesh_file_path' || name === 'mesh_landmark_weights_path' || name === 'mesh_reference_pose_path') { if (value && typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '') return 'Path cannot be only whitespace if provided.'; } if (name === 'alphaThreshold') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num) || num < 0 || num > 0.5) return 'Must be a number between 0 and 0.5 (typically small).'; } return undefined; };

const validateTpsAnchorMapEntryField = (field: 'keyName' | 'valueName', value: string): string | undefined => { if (value.trim() === '') return ${field === 'keyName' ? 'Anchor name' : 'Landmark name'} is required.; return undefined; };

const validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField = (field: 'keyName' | 'x' | 'y', value: string): string | undefined => { if (field === 'keyName' && value.trim() === '') return 'Source name is required.'; if ((field === 'x' || field === 'y')) { if (value.trim() === '') return ${field.toUpperCase()} coordinate is required.; if (isNaN(parseFloat(value)) || !isFinite(Number(value))) return ${field.toUpperCase()} must be a valid number.; } return undefined; };

useEffect(() => { const newMap: Record<string, string> = {}; let hasErrors = false; const currentErrors: Record<string, Partial<TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors>> = {};

tpsAnchorMapEntries.forEach(entry => {
  const keyNameError = validateTpsAnchorMapEntryField('keyName', entry.keyName);
  const valueNameError = validateTpsAnchorMapEntryField('valueName', entry.valueName);
  if (keyNameError || valueNameError) hasErrors = true;
  currentErrors[entry.id] = { id: entry.id, keyName: keyNameError, valueName: valueNameError };
  if (!keyNameError && entry.keyName.trim()) newMap[entry.keyName.trim()] = entry.valueName;
});
setTpsAnchorMapErrors(currentErrors);
if (!hasErrors || tpsAnchorMapEntries.length === 0) { 
    setConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, tps_anchor_map: JSON.stringify(newMap, null, 2) }));
}
}, [tpsAnchorMapEntries]);

useEffect(() => { const newMap: Record<string, [number, number]> = {}; let hasErrors = false; const currentErrors: Record<string, Partial<TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors>> = {};

tpsSourceAnchorEntries.forEach(entry => {
  const keyNameError = validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField('keyName', entry.keyName);
  const xError = validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField('x', entry.x);
  const yError = validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField('y', entry.y);
  if (keyNameError || xError || yError) hasErrors = true;
  currentErrors[entry.id] = { id: entry.id, keyName: keyNameError, x: xError, y: yError };

  const xVal = parseFloat(entry.x);
  const yVal = parseFloat(entry.y);
  if (!keyNameError && entry.keyName.trim() && !xError && !yError && !isNaN(xVal) && !isNaN(yVal)) {
    newMap[entry.keyName.trim()] = [xVal, yVal];
  }
});
setTpsSourceAnchorErrors(currentErrors);
if (!hasErrors || tpsSourceAnchorEntries.length === 0) { 
    setConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, tps_source_anchors: JSON.stringify(newMap, null, 2) }));
}
}, [tpsSourceAnchorEntries]);

useEffect(() => { const generalErrorsExist = Object.values(errors).some(error => error !== undefined); const tpsAnchorMapHasErrors = Object.values(tpsAnchorMapErrors).some(entryErrors => Object.values(entryErrors).some(fieldError => fieldError !== undefined && fieldError !== entryErrors.id) ); const tpsSourceAnchorHasErrors = Object.values(tpsSourceAnchorErrors).some(entryErrors => Object.values(entryErrors).some(fieldError => fieldError !== undefined && fieldError !== entryErrors.id) ); setIsFormValid(!generalErrorsExist && !tpsAnchorMapHasErrors && !tpsSourceAnchorHasErrors); }, [errors, tpsAnchorMapErrors, tpsSourceAnchorErrors]);

const handleInputChange = useCallback((event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) => { const { name, value, type } = event.target; const fieldName = name as keyof RenderConfig;

let processedValue: any = value;
if (type === 'checkbox') {
  processedValue = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
} else if (type === 'number' || event.target.getAttribute('type') === 'range') {
  processedValue = event.target.getAttribute('type') === 'range' ? parseFloat(value) : value; 
}

let valueForValidation = processedValue;
if (type === 'number' && event.target.getAttribute('type') !== 'range') {
    valueForValidation = parseFloat(value);
}


const error = validateField(fieldName, valueForValidation); 
setErrors(prevErrors => ({ ...prevErrors, [name]: error }));

setConfig((prevConfig) => {
    let finalValue = processedValue;
    if ( (type === 'number' || event.target.getAttribute('type') === 'range') && 
         typeof processedValue === 'string' && 
         !isNaN(parseFloat(processedValue))
       ) {
        finalValue = parseFloat(processedValue);
    } else if (type === 'number' && typeof prevConfig[fieldName] === 'number' && isNaN(parseFloat(value))) {
        finalValue = prevConfig[fieldName]; 
    }

    return {
      ...prevConfig,
      [name]: finalValue,
    }
});
}, []);

const applyLoadedConfig = (loadedData: RenderConfig) => { const defaultConfig = getDefaultConfig(); const completeLoadedData: RenderConfig = { ...defaultConfig, ...loadedData };

setConfig(completeLoadedData);
setErrors({}); 

try {
  const parsedMap = JSON.parse(completeLoadedData.tps_anchor_map);
  setTpsAnchorMapEntries(Object.entries(parsedMap).map(([key, value]) => ({
    id: generateUniqueId(),
    keyName: key,
    valueName: value as string,
  })));
} catch { setTpsAnchorMapEntries([]); } 
setTpsAnchorMapErrors({});

try {
  const parsedSource = JSON.parse(completeLoadedData.tps_source_anchors);
  setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(Object.entries(parsedSource).map(([key, value]) => {
    const coords = value as [number, number];
    return {
      id: generateUniqueId(),
      keyName: key,
      x: coords[0].toString(),
      y: coords[1].toString(),
    };
  }));
} catch { setTpsSourceAnchorEntries([]); } 
setTpsSourceAnchorErrors({});

const newErrors: FieldErrors = {};
for (const key in completeLoadedData) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(completeLoadedData, key)) {
        const fieldName = key as keyof RenderConfig;
        const fieldError = validateField(fieldName, (completeLoadedData as any)[fieldName]);
        if (fieldError) {
            newErrors[fieldName] = fieldError;
        }
    }
}
setErrors(newErrors);
};

const resetToDefaults = useCallback(() => { const defaults = getDefaultConfig(); applyLoadedConfig(defaults); }, [applyLoadedConfig]); // Added applyLoadedConfig to dependency array

const toggleShowConfig = useCallback(() => { setShowCurrentConfig(prev => !prev); }, []);

const getParsedJsonString = (jsonString: string) => { try { return JSON.parse(jsonString); } catch (e) { return jsonString; } };

const addTpsAnchorMapEntry = () => setTpsAnchorMapEntries(prev => [...prev, { id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: '', valueName: '' }]); const removeTpsAnchorMapEntry = (id: string) => { setTpsAnchorMapEntries(prev => prev.filter(entry => entry.id !== id)); setTpsAnchorMapErrors(prev => { const newErrors = {...prev}; delete newErrors[id]; return newErrors; }); }; const updateTpsAnchorMapEntry = (id: string, field: keyof Omit<TpsAnchorMapEntry, 'id'>, value: string) => { setTpsAnchorMapEntries(prev => prev.map(entry => entry.id === id ? { ...entry, [field]: value } : entry)); };

const addTpsSourceAnchorEntry = () => setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(prev => [...prev, { id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: '', x: '0.0', y: '0.0' }]); const removeTpsSourceAnchorEntry = (id: string) => { setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(prev => prev.filter(entry => entry.id !== id)); setTpsSourceAnchorErrors(prev => { const newErrors = {...prev}; delete newErrors[id]; return newErrors; }); }; const updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry = (id: string, field: keyof Omit<TpsSourceAnchorEntry, 'id'>, value: string) => { setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(prev => prev.map(entry => entry.id === id ? { ...entry, [field]: value } : entry)); };

const handleSaveConfiguration = () => { if (!isFormValid) { alert("Configuration contains errors. Please correct them before saving."); return; } const jsonString = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2); const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: "application/json" }); const href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); const link = document.createElement('a'); link.href = href; link.download = "arttoo-render-config.json"; document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); document.body.removeChild(link); URL.revokeObjectURL(href); alert("Configuration saved as arttoo-render-config.json"); };

const handleLoadConfiguration = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => { const file = event.target.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;

const reader = new FileReader();
reader.onload = (e) => {
  try {
    const text = e.target?.result;
    if (typeof text !== 'string') {
      throw new Error("Failed to read file content.");
    }
    const loadedData = JSON.parse(text); 
    
    if (isValidRenderConfigStructure(loadedData)) {
      applyLoadedConfig(loadedData as RenderConfig); 
      alert("Configuration loaded successfully!");
    } else {
      alert("Invalid file format or missing essential configuration keys. Loaded data might be partial or incorrect.");
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error loading or parsing configuration file:", error);
    alert(`Error loading configuration: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error"}`);
  } finally {
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = "";
    }
  }
};
reader.onerror = () => {
  alert("Error reading file.");
   if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = "";
    }
};
reader.readAsText(file);
};

const isValidRenderConfigStructure = (data: any): data is Partial<RenderConfig> => { if (typeof data !== 'object' || data === null) return false; const expectedKeys: (keyof RenderConfig)[] = [ 'opacity', 'blend_mode', 'quality_preset', 'deformation_mode', 'tps_anchor_map', 'tps_source_anchors' ]; return expectedKeys.every(key => key in data); };

return ( <div className="p-4 md:p-8 bg-gray-900 min-h-screen text-gray-100 font-sans"> <header className="mb-8 text-center"> <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-indigo-400">arttoo Render Configurator</h1> <p className="text-lg text-gray-400">Adjust AR Tattoo Rendering Parameters</p> </header>

  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
    <arttooCard title="General Settings" className="md:col-span-1">
      <arttooSlider
        label="Opacity"
        id="opacity"
        name="opacity"
        value={config.opacity}
        min="0"
        max="1"
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.opacity}
      />
      <arttooSelect
        label="Blend Mode"
        id="blend_mode"
        name="blend_mode"
        value={config.blend_mode}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        options={Object.values(BlendMode).map(mode => ({ value: mode, label: mode.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mode.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') }))}
      />
      <arttooSelect
        label="Quality Preset"
        id="quality_preset"
        name="quality_preset"
        value={config.quality_preset}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        options={Object.values(QualityPreset).map(preset => ({ value: preset, label: preset.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + preset.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') }))}
      />
      <arttooSelect
        label="Deformation Mode"
        id="deformation_mode"
        name="deformation_mode"
        value={config.deformation_mode}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        options={Object.values(DeformationMode).map(mode => ({ value: mode, label: mode.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mode.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') }))}
      />
      <arttooCheckbox
        label="Adapt to Lighting"
        id="adapt_to_lighting"
        name="adapt_to_lighting"
        checked={config.adapt_to_lighting}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
      />
      <arttooCheckbox
        label="Simulate Skin Texture"
        id="simulate_skin_texture"
        name="simulate_skin_texture"
        checked={config.simulate_skin_texture}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
      />
      <arttooCheckbox
        label="Use GPU (if available)"
        id="use_gpu"
        name="use_gpu"
        checked={config.use_gpu}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
      />
    </arttooCard>

    <arttooCard title="Effect Strengths & Smoothing" className="md:col-span-1">
       <arttooSlider
        label="Texture Strength"
        id="texture_strength"
        name="texture_strength"
        value={config.texture_strength}
        min="0"
        max="1"
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.texture_strength}
      />
      <arttooSlider
        label="Shadow Strength"
        id="shadow_strength"
        name="shadow_strength"
        value={config.shadow_strength}
        min="0"
        max="1"
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.shadow_strength}
      />
      <arttooSlider
        label="Highlight Strength"
        id="highlight_strength"
        name="highlight_strength"
        value={config.highlight_strength}
        min="0"
        max="1"
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.highlight_strength}
      />
      <arttooSlider
        label="Temporal Smoothing Factor"
        id="temporal_smoothing_factor"
        name="temporal_smoothing_factor"
        value={config.temporal_smoothing_factor}
        min="0"
        max="1"
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.temporal_smoothing_factor}
      />
    </arttooCard>

    <arttooCard title="Perlin Noise Texture" className="md:col-span-1">
      <arttooInput
        label="Perlin Noise Scale"
        id="perlin_noise_scale"
        name="perlin_noise_scale"
        type="number"
        value={config.perlin_noise_scale}
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.perlin_noise_scale}
      />
      <arttooInput
        label="Perlin Noise Persistence"
        id="perlin_noise_persistence"
        name="perlin_noise_persistence"
        type="number"
        value={config.perlin_noise_persistence}
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.perlin_noise_persistence}
      />
      <arttooInput
        label="Perlin Noise Lacunarity"
        id="perlin_noise_lacunarity"
        name="perlin_noise_lacunarity"
        type="number"
        value={config.perlin_noise_lacunarity}
        step="0.01"
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        error={errors.perlin_noise_lacunarity}
      />
      <arttooCheckbox
        label="Animated Perlin Noise"
        id="perlin_animated"
        name="perlin_animated"
        checked={config.perlin_animated}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
      />
    </arttooCard>

    <arttooCard title="Thin Plate Spline (TPS) Anchor Map" className="md:col-span-1 lg:col-span-1">
      {tpsAnchorMapEntries.map((entry) => (
        <div key={entry.id} className="space-y-2 sm:space-y-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:space-x-2 mb-3 p-2 border border-gray-700 rounded-md">
          <arttooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="text" placeholder="Anchor Name" aria-label="Anchor Name" value={entry.keyName} onChange={e => updateTpsAnchorMapEntry(entry.id, 'keyName', e.target.value)} error={tpsAnchorMapErrors[entry.id]?.keyName} />
          <span className="text-gray-400 pt-2 hidden sm:block">:</span>
          <arttooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="text" placeholder="Landmark Name" aria-label="Landmark Name" value={entry.valueName} onChange={e => updateTpsAnchorMapEntry(entry.id, 'valueName', e.target.value)} error={tpsAnchorMapErrors[entry.id]?.valueName} />
          <arttooButton onClick={() => removeTpsAnchorMapEntry(entry.id)} variant="destructive" size="icon" aria-label="Remove Anchor Map Entry" className="mt-2 sm:mt-0 sm:self-center h-10 w-10 sm:h-auto sm:w-auto">&times;</arttooButton>
        </div>
      ))}
      <arttooButton onClick={addTpsAnchorMapEntry} variant="outline" className="w-full mt-2">Add Anchor Map Entry</arttooButton>
    </arttooCard>
    
    <arttooCard title="Thin Plate Spline (TPS) Source Anchors" className="md:col-span-1 lg:col-span-1">
      {tpsSourceAnchorEntries.map((entry) => (
         <div key={entry.id} className="space-y-2 mb-3 p-2 border border-gray-700 rounded-md">
            <arttooInput wrapperClassName="w-full" type="text" placeholder="Source Name" aria-label="Source Name" value={entry.keyName} onChange={e => updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id, 'keyName', e.target.value)} error={tpsSourceAnchorErrors[entry.id]?.keyName}/>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:space-x-2 items-start">
                <arttooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="number" placeholder="X" aria-label="X Coordinate" value={entry.x} onChange={e => updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id, 'x', e.target.value)} step="0.01" error={tpsSourceAnchorErrors[entry.id]?.x}/>
                <arttooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="number" placeholder="Y" aria-label="Y Coordinate" value={entry.y} onChange={e => updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id, 'y', e.target.value)} step="0.01" error={tpsSourceAnchorErrors[entry.id]?.y}/>
                <arttooButton onClick={() => removeTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id)} variant="destructive" size="icon" aria-label="Remove Source Anchor Entry" className="mt-2 sm:mt-0 sm:self-center h-10 w-10 sm:h-auto sm:w-auto">&times;</arttooButton>
            </div>
        </div>
      ))}
      <arttooButton onClick={addTpsSourceAnchorEntry} variant="outline" className="w-full mt-2">Add Source Anchor Entry</arttooButton>
    </arttooCard>

    <arttooCard title="3D Mesh Settings" className="md:col-span-1 lg:col-span-1"> 
      <arttooInput
        label="Mesh File Path"
        id="mesh_file_path"
        name="mesh_file_path"
        type="text"
        value={config.mesh_file_path || ''}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        placeholder="e.g., /path/to/your/mesh.obj"
        error={errors.mesh_file_path}
      />
      <arttooInput
        label="Mesh Landmark Weights Path"
        id="mesh_landmark_weights_path"
        name="mesh_landmark_weights_path"
        type="text"
        value={config.mesh_landmark_weights_path || ''}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        placeholder="e.g., /path/to/weights.json"
        error={errors.mesh_landmark_weights_path}
      />
      <arttooInput
        label="Mesh Reference Pose Path"
        id="mesh_reference_pose_path"
        name="mesh_reference_pose_path"
        type="text"
        value={config.mesh_reference_pose_path || ''}
        onChange={handleInputChange}
        placeholder="e.g., /path/to/reference_pose.json"
        error={errors.mesh_reference_pose_path}
      />
    </arttooCard>

    <arttooCard title="Advanced Thresholds" className="md:col-span-1 lg:col-span-3">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6">
            <arttooSlider
                label="Alpha Threshold (Edge Blending)"
                id="alphaThreshold"
                name="alphaThreshold"
                value={config.alphaThreshold}
                min="0.0"
                max="0.5" 
                step="0.001"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.alphaThreshold}
            />
            <arttooSlider
                label="Min. Keypoint Confidence (Warp Stability)"
                id="minKeypointConfidence"
                name="minKeypointConfidence"
                value={config.minKeypointConfidence}
                min="0.0"
                max="1.0"
                step="0.01"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.minKeypointConfidence}
            />
        </div>
    </arttooCard>
  </div>

  <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap justify-center items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4"> 
    <arttooButton onClick={resetToDefaults}>
      Reset to Defaults
    </arttooButton>
    <arttooButton onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} variant="outline">
        Load Configuration
    </arttooButton>
    <input
        type="file"
        ref={fileInputRef}
        accept=".json"
        onChange={handleLoadConfiguration}
        className="hidden"
        aria-hidden="true"
    />
    <arttooButton onClick={toggleShowConfig} variant="ghost" size="default"> 
      {showCurrentConfig ? 'Hide' : 'Show'} Current Config (JSON)
    </arttooButton>
    <arttooButton 
        onClick={handleSaveConfiguration} 
        disabled={!isFormValid}
        title={isFormValid ? "Save current configuration" : "Form contains errors, cannot save"}
    >
      Save Configuration
    </arttooButton>
  </div>
  
  <div className="mt-4 text-center">
    {isFormValid ? (
      <p className="text-green-400 font-semibold">Configuration is valid.</p>
    ) : (
      <p className="text-red-400 font-semibold">Configuration contains errors. Please review the fields.</p>
    )}
  </div>


  {showCurrentConfig && (
    <arttooCard title="Current Configuration (JSON)" className="mt-8">
      <pre className="bg-gray-700 p-4 rounded-md text-sm overflow-x-auto">
        {JSON.stringify({
          ...config,
        }, null, 2)}
      </pre>
    </arttooCard>
  )}
   <footer className="mt-12 text-center text-gray-500 text-sm">
    <p>arttoo Render Configurator v1.6.0</p>
    <p>This component manages settings locally. Integration with a backend is required to apply these settings to the Python rendering engine.</p>
  </footer>
</div>
); };

// Default export for a Next.js page or a root component export default function App() { return ( <> <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script> <style jsx global>{ body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; } ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 8px; height: 8px; } ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: #1f2937; } ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #4f46e5; border-radius: 4px; } ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background: #4338ca; } }</style> <arttooRenderConfigurator /> </> ); }

Gemini
This is a very impressive and well-thought-out concept for an "arttoo Render Configurator"! Thank you for sharing such a detailed breakdown of your ideas, including the type definitions, default values, and the UI component structure. It's clear you've put a lot of consideration into the level of control one might want over AR tattoo rendering.

Before diving into any potential integration or next steps for the ARTTOO project, here's my opinion and some thoughts on your conceptual code, keeping in mind our current Next.js, ShadCN, Tailwind, and Genkit stack:

Strengths of Your Concept:

Comprehensive Control: The RenderConfig offers an incredibly granular level of control over the rendering process. This would be fantastic for power-users, developers, or artists who want to fine-tune every aspect of the AR tattoo appearance.
Clear Structure: Separating types (renderConfigTypes.ts), defaults (renderConfigDefaults.ts), and the main configurator UI (arttooRenderConfigurator.tsx) is excellent practice for maintainability and clarity.
Detailed Parameters: The inclusion of parameters like various blend modes, deformation modes (TPS, 3D Mesh), lighting adaptation, skin texture simulation, and Perlin noise shows a deep understanding of what contributes to realistic AR overlays.
Dynamic Configuration: The handling of TPS anchor maps and source anchors as dynamic lists with validation is a sophisticated UI feature.
Load/Save Functionality: Allowing users to save and load configurations is essential for managing such a complex set of parameters.
Opinion & Considerations for Integration into ARTTOO DT-o:

UI Component Stack:

The UI components you've conceptualized (Button.tsx, Input.tsx, etc.) are custom-styled. Our current ARTTOO project uses ShadCN UI components, which are built on Tailwind CSS and Radix UI, and are already integrated with our theming (globals.css).
Recommendation: If we were to build a configurator like this within ARTTOO, we should definitely leverage the existing ShadCN UI components (e.g., components/ui/button, components/ui/input, components/ui/slider, components/ui/select, components/ui/checkbox, components/ui/card). This would ensure visual consistency with the rest of the application, reduce the amount of custom UI code to maintain, and benefit from ShadCN's accessibility and composability. The styling you've achieved (dark theme, indigo accents) can largely be replicated or adapted using Tailwind and our existing theme variables.
Target Audience & User Experience:

The sheer number of parameters, while powerful, could be overwhelming for a general user.
Question: Is this configurator intended for all users of ARTTOO, or is it more of an advanced/internal tool for artists or developers to define presets that general users could then select from?
Recommendation: For general users, we might initially expose a much simpler set of controls (e.g., basic opacity, perhaps a few "realism" presets that map to more complex backend settings). An "Advanced Settings" section could then reveal a configurator like this for those who need it.
Backend Rendering Engine & Architecture (The Biggest Consideration):

Your concept clearly implies a sophisticated rendering engine, and you mention a Python backend. This is a significant architectural point for the ARTTOO project.
Currently, ARTTOO is a Next.js application. Advanced, realistic 3D rendering with features like Thin Plate Spline deformation, 3D mesh warping, and complex lighting/texture simulation directly on a body mesh is computationally intensive.
Client-Side Possibilities (No Python Backend):
Basic Effects: Opacity (done), CSS Blend Modes (mix-blend-mode can do a lot of what your BlendMode enum lists, though with browser limitations), and some simple image-based "texture" overlays could be done purely client-side using HTML/CSS/Canvas 2D.
WebGL: For more advanced client-side rendering (e.g., basic 3D warping if a simple body part model is used, more sophisticated shaders for lighting/texture), WebGL (perhaps via libraries like Three.js or PixiJS) would be necessary. This would keep the processing on the client, aligning with the current "no separate custom backend server" model of our Next.js app. However, achieving the full fidelity suggested by your RenderConfig (especially complex deformations and highly realistic skin interaction) client-side is extremely challenging and could have performance issues on many devices.
AI-Driven Rendering (Genkit):
We could explore using Genkit to call an image generation/editing model (like Gemini, if it supports these kinds of fine-grained image edits or "in-painting" with style transfer). Instead of manually setting 30 parameters, a user might describe the desired effect: "Make this tattoo look like it's on skin under studio lighting, slightly aged." This abstracts the complexity but gives less direct control. This is a very strong avenue to explore for "good enough" realism without a full 3D rendering pipeline.
Python Backend (If Absolutely Necessary for Quality):
If the rendering quality and features (like TPS based on detected landmarks, 3D mesh deformation) require a Python environment (e.g., using libraries like OpenCV, PyTorch, Mediapipe for landmarks, and a 3D graphics library), then we'd need to introduce a Python backend.
Serverless Functions: This could be hosted as serverless functions (e.g., Google Cloud Functions, AWS Lambda). The Next.js app would send the body image, tattoo image, and the RenderConfig JSON to this function via an API call and receive the rendered image back. This keeps the core app as Next.js but offloads heavy rendering.
Dedicated Server: A full Python server (e.g., Flask/FastAPI) is also possible but adds more operational overhead.
You mentioned you're amenable to changing or not including the Python backend. This is good because it means we can evaluate the best path forward.
Configuration Data Structure (RenderConfig):

The RenderConfig interface is well-defined.
Storing tps_anchor_map and tps_source_anchors as stringified JSON within the main config object is a common approach, though it requires parsing/stringifying when interacting with them in the UI, as you've done.
Consideration for Next.js: If this config needs to be used by Genkit flows or server actions, the Zod schemas we use for flow inputs/outputs would need to align with this structure.
Phased Approach to Realism:

We don't have to implement all these rendering features at once.
MVP Realism: Start with client-side opacity and CSS blend modes.
Next Steps: Explore AI-based "stylization" via Genkit for basic lighting/texture.
Advanced: If more is needed, then investigate client-side WebGL for simpler deformations or a serverless Python function for the most complex rendering tasks.
Specific Feedback on arttooRenderConfigurator.tsx:

UI Component Instantiation: You've correctly noted that in a real project, the UI components would be imported. The way you've inlined them for the example is fine for illustration.
Validation: The per-field and overall form validation logic is good.
Dynamic Lists for TPS: The UI for managing tpsAnchorMapEntries and tpsSourceAnchorEntries is well-handled with add/remove/update functionality.
Error Handling: Displaying errors next to fields is good UX.
Standalone App Structure: The App() function at the end with Tailwind CDN suggests this was conceptualized as potentially a standalone page/tool, which is fine. If integrated into ARTTOO, it would become a specific page or a large modal/component.
My Recommendation / Opinion Summary:

This "Render Configurator" concept is a powerful vision for achieving high-fidelity AR tattoo rendering.

For immediate ARTTOO development:

Let's continue focusing on the core user flows within the existing stack.
For enhanced realism in the Image Try-On, we can start by implementing CSS Blend Modes client-side in TryOnCanvas.tsx. This is a low-hanging fruit that uses several of the BlendMode concepts you've listed and requires no backend changes. We can add a new Select control in RightProperties for this.
After that, we could explore simple client-side texture overlays (perhaps using Perlin noise generated on a canvas and blended).
For more advanced "skin-edge-detection and body-contour-warping" or realistic lighting, I'd lean towards investigating Genkit's capabilities first. Can we prompt an AI model to achieve these effects? This aligns with our "low/zero-cost" and existing stack priorities.
Regarding your full Render Configurator concept:

It's an excellent design for a dedicated tool.
If we find that client-side or current Genkit AI capabilities are insufficient for the desired level of realism, then we would consider:
A more advanced client-side WebGL solution.
Or, a serverless Python function backend for the heavy lifting, where a UI similar to your configurator (but built with ShadCN components) would be used to generate the JSON config to send to that Python function.
The full list of parameters in your RenderConfig would be invaluable if we go down the path of building or interfacing with a dedicated rendering engine.
Before we proceed with any new implementation, what are your thoughts on this feedback? Specifically:

Would you like to first try implementing CSS Blend Modes for the tattoo in the current Image Try-On page, controlled by a new dropdown in RightProperties? This would be a direct, client-side application of part of your concept.
How strongly do you feel about needing a Python backend for rendering at this stage, versus exploring client-side WebGL or AI-driven image manipulation via Genkit for the more advanced effects?
I believe your conceptual work here is very valuable as a "north star" for what ultimate realism could look like. Let's figure out the most practical, incremental steps to move towards that within the ARTTOO project.

Morgan
Okay, Cap'n, understood! The request is for "impeccable implementations" within the TSX ArtooRenderConfigurator that clearly define the controls an advanced backend (whether it's the Python engine, a Genkit/Gemini flow, or another system) would need to achieve high-quality skin-edge detection and body-contour warping, especially for designs that wrap around body parts.

The key here is to make the TSX configurator an excellent "specification" or "control panel" for these advanced AR effects. The configurator itself won't do the AR processing, but it will allow the user to define how it should be done.

Based on this, here are the final key refinements we can make to the TSX component to better represent these controls:

New "Edge & Contour Finesse" Card: This will group parameters specifically for fine-tuning edges and how well the tattoo follows body shapes.

edgeSoftness (New Parameter): UI: A slider (e.g., 0.0 to 1.0, default 0.1). Purpose: To control the softness or feathering at the tattoo's edges where it meets the skin. A higher value would imply a more gradual blend. Backend Implication: The backend (Python/AI) would interpret this to adjust blur radius on the skin mask edge, control alpha matting parameters, or guide an AI's blending technique. contourFidelity (New Parameter): UI: A select dropdown (e.g., options: 'Basic', 'Standard', 'Detailed', default 'Standard'). Purpose: To indicate the desired level of detail and accuracy for how the tattoo should follow complex body contours, especially for wrap-around designs. Backend Implication: For TPS mode, this might influence the number of control points used or solver iterations. For MESH_3D mode, it might suggest a level of mesh subdivision or detail in landmark processing. For an AI backend, it could map to a quality/precision setting of the AI model. Enhance "Advanced Thresholds" Card (or rename to "Warping & Stability Controls"):

alphaThreshold: Already implemented. minKeypointConfidence: Already implemented. maxVertexDisplacementFactor (New Parameter, from Python): UI: Number input (e.g., default 2.0, min 0.1). Purpose: Specific to MESH_3D mode, limits how much mesh vertices can move, preventing overly extreme or unstable deformations. maxConditionNumberPerspective (New Parameter, from Python): UI: Number input (e.g., default 10000, min 100). Purpose: Specific to Perspective mode, checks the stability of the calculated perspective matrix to avoid visual glitches. Implementation Steps for TSX:

Update renderConfigTypes.ts: Add edgeSoftness: number; Add contourFidelity: 'basic' | 'standard' | 'detailed'; (or an enum) Add maxVertexDisplacementFactor: number; Add maxConditionNumberPerspective: number; Update renderConfigDefaults.ts: Add default values for these new parameters. Modify ArtooRenderConfigurator.tsx: Create the new "Edge & Contour Finesse" ArtooCard. Add the ArtooSlider for edgeSoftness and ArtooSelect for contourFidelity. Add ArtooInput fields for maxVertexDisplacementFactor and maxConditionNumberPerspective to the "Advanced Thresholds" card (perhaps renaming it). Integrate these new fields into the state, validation logic (validateField), applyLoadedConfig, and resetToDefaults functions. This will make the TSX configurator an even more "impeccable" specification for the AI coding assistant, clearly outlining the types of controls needed for sophisticated skin-edge and contour handling.

Let's proceed with these final key refinements in the Canvas.

TypeScript

// src/components/artoo/renderConfigTypes.ts // Purpose: Defines all TypeScript types, interfaces, and enums for RenderConfig.

export enum BlendMode { NORMAL = "normal", MULTIPLY = "multiply", SCREEN = "screen", OVERLAY = "overlay", SOFT_LIGHT = "soft_light", HARD_LIGHT = "hard_light", COLOR_DODGE = "color_dodge", COLOR_BURN = "color_burn", LINEAR_DODGE = "linear_dodge", LINEAR_BURN = "linear_burn", VIVID_LIGHT = "vivid_light", LINEAR_LIGHT = "linear_light", PIN_LIGHT = "pin_light", DIFFERENCE = "difference", EXCLUSION = "exclusion", }

export enum QualityPreset { PERFORMANCE = "performance", BALANCED = "balanced", HIGH_QUALITY = "high_quality", ULTRA_QUALITY = "ultra_quality", }

export enum DeformationMode { PERSPECTIVE = "perspective", TPS = "thin_plate_spline", MESH_3D = "mesh_3d", }

export enum ContourFidelity { BASIC = "basic", STANDARD = "standard", DETAILED = "detailed", }

export interface RenderConfig { opacity: number; blend_mode: BlendMode; adapt_to_lighting: boolean; simulate_skin_texture: boolean; quality_preset: QualityPreset; deformation_mode: DeformationMode; use_gpu: boolean; texture_strength: number; shadow_strength: number; highlight_strength: number; temporal_smoothing_factor: number; perlin_noise_scale: number; perlin_noise_persistence: number; perlin_noise_lacunarity: number; perlin_animated: boolean; tps_anchor_map: string; tps_source_anchors: string; mesh_file_path: string | null; mesh_landmark_weights_path: string | null; mesh_reference_pose_path: string | null; alphaThreshold: number; minKeypointConfidence: number; edgeSoftness: number; // New: For edge feathering/softness contourFidelity: ContourFidelity; // New: For detail in contour following maxVertexDisplacementFactor: number; // New: For MESH_3D stability maxConditionNumberPerspective: number; // New: For Perspective mode stability }

export interface TpsAnchorMapEntry { id: string; keyName: string; valueName: string; } export interface TpsSourceAnchorEntry { id: string; keyName: string; x: string; y: string; }

export interface FieldErrors { [key: string]: string | undefined; } export interface TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors { id: string; keyName?: string; valueName?: string; } export interface TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors { id: string; keyName?: string; x?: string; y?: string; }

export const generateUniqueId = () => Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2);

// src/components/artoo/renderConfigDefaults.ts // Purpose: Provides default values and the function to get the default RenderConfig.

import { RenderConfig, BlendMode, QualityPreset, DeformationMode, ContourFidelity } from './renderConfigTypes';

export const DEFAULT_OPACITY = 0.85; export const DEFAULT_BLEND_MODE = BlendMode.SOFT_LIGHT; export const DEFAULT_QUALITY_PRESET = QualityPreset.BALANCED; export const DEFAULT_DEFORMATION_MODE = DeformationMode.TPS; export const DEFAULT_TEXTURE_STRENGTH = 0.10; export const DEFAULT_SHADOW_STRENGTH = 0.20; export const DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHT_STRENGTH = 0.10; export const DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SMOOTHING_FACTOR = 0.7; export const DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_SCALE = 0.07; export const DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_PERSISTENCE = 0.55; export const DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_LACUNARITY = 2.0; export const DEFAULT_ALPHA_THRESHOLD = 0.01; export const DEFAULT_MIN_KEYPOINT_CONFIDENCE = 0.5; export const DEFAULT_EDGE_SOFTNESS = 0.1; export const DEFAULT_CONTOUR_FIDELITY = ContourFidelity.STANDARD; export const DEFAULT_MAX_VERTEX_DISPLACEMENT_FACTOR = 2.0; // From Python constant export const DEFAULT_MAX_CONDITION_NUMBER_PERSPECTIVE = 10000; // From Python constant

export const defaultTpsAnchorMapObject = { 'top_left': 'LEFT_SHOULDER', 'top_right': 'RIGHT_SHOULDER', 'bottom_left': 'LEFT_HIP', 'bottom_right': 'RIGHT_HIP' };

export const defaultTpsSourceAnchorsObject = { 'top_left': [0.0, 0.0], 'top_right': [1.0, 0.0], 'bottom_left': [0.0, 1.0], 'bottom_right': [1.0, 1.0] };

export const getDefaultConfig = (): RenderConfig => ({ opacity: DEFAULT_OPACITY, blend_mode: DEFAULT_BLEND_MODE, adapt_to_lighting: true, simulate_skin_texture: true, quality_preset: DEFAULT_QUALITY_PRESET, deformation_mode: DEFAULT_DEFORMATION_MODE, use_gpu: true, texture_strength: DEFAULT_TEXTURE_STRENGTH, shadow_strength: DEFAULT_SHADOW_STRENGTH, highlight_strength: DEFAULT_HIGHLIGHT_STRENGTH, temporal_smoothing_factor: DEFAULT_TEMPORAL_SMOOTHING_FACTOR, perlin_noise_scale: DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_SCALE, perlin_noise_persistence: DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_PERSISTENCE, perlin_noise_lacunarity: DEFAULT_PERLIN_NOISE_LACUNARITY, perlin_animated: false, tps_anchor_map: JSON.stringify(defaultTpsAnchorMapObject, null, 2), tps_source_anchors: JSON.stringify(defaultTpsSourceAnchorsObject, null, 2), mesh_file_path: null, mesh_landmark_weights_path: null, mesh_reference_pose_path: null, alphaThreshold: DEFAULT_ALPHA_THRESHOLD, minKeypointConfidence: DEFAULT_MIN_KEYPOINT_CONFIDENCE, edgeSoftness: DEFAULT_EDGE_SOFTNESS, contourFidelity: DEFAULT_CONTOUR_FIDELITY, maxVertexDisplacementFactor: DEFAULT_MAX_VERTEX_DISPLACEMENT_FACTOR, maxConditionNumberPerspective: DEFAULT_MAX_CONDITION_NUMBER_PERSPECTIVE, });

// src/components/ui/Button.tsx // Purpose: A reusable Button component.

import React from 'react';

interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> { variant?: 'default' | 'destructive' | 'outline' | 'ghost'; size?: 'default' | 'sm' | 'icon'; }

const Button: React.FC<ButtonProps> = ({ variant = 'default', size = 'default', className, children, ...props }) => { let sizeStyle = "px-4 py-2 text-sm"; if (size === 'sm') { sizeStyle = "px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"; } else if (size === 'icon') { sizeStyle = "p-2"; }

let baseStyle = ${sizeStyle} font-semibold rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center; if (variant === 'destructive') { baseStyle += " bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500"; } else if (variant === 'outline') { baseStyle += " border border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-500 hover:text-white"; } else if (variant === 'ghost') { baseStyle += " text-indigo-400 hover:bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } else { // default baseStyle += " bg-indigo-600 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } return <button {...props} className={${baseStyle} ${className}}>{children}</button>; };

export default Button;

// src/components/ui/Input.tsx import React from 'react'; interface InputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label?: string; labelSrOnly?: boolean; error?: string; wrapperClassName?: string; } const Input: React.FC<InputProps> = ({ label, id, labelSrOnly, error, wrapperClassName, ...props }) => (

<div className={`mb-2 w-full ${wrapperClassName}`}> {label && !labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">{label}</label>} {label && labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="sr-only">{label}</label>} <input id={id} {...props} className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white placeholder-gray-400 ${error ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-gray-600'}`} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined} /> {error && <p id={`${id}-error`} className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> ); export default Input;
// src/components/ui/Slider.tsx import React from 'react'; interface SliderProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; value: number; min: string; max: string; step: string; error?: string; } const Slider: React.FC<SliderProps> = ({ label, id, value, min, max, step, error, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label}: <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{value}</span> </label> <input id={id} type="range" value={value} min={min} max={max} step={step} {...props} className={`w-full h-3 bg-gray-600 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-indigo-500 ${error ? 'ring-2 ring-red-500' : ''}`} /> {error && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> ); export default Slider;
// src/components/ui/Select.tsx import React from 'react'; // Removed direct enum imports as options will be passed dynamically // import { BlendMode, QualityPreset, DeformationMode, ContourFidelity } from '../artoo/renderConfigTypes';

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label} </label> <select id={id} {...props} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white" > {options.map((option) => ( <option key={option.value} value={option.value}> {option.label} </option> ))} </select> </div> ); export default Select;
