// src/components/artoo/ArtooRenderConfigurator.tsx

import React, { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'; import { RenderConfig, BlendMode, QualityPreset, DeformationMode, ContourFidelity, TpsAnchorMapEntry, TpsSourceAnchorEntry, FieldErrors, TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors, TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors, generateUniqueId } from './renderConfigTypes'; import { getDefaultConfig } from './renderConfigDefaults';

// --- Re-define UI components here for Canvas example --- // In a real project: import ArtooButton from '../ui/Button'; import ArtooInput from '../ui/Input'; etc. // For simplicity in Canvas, using Artoo prefixed names for inlined components.

interface ArtooInputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label?: string; labelSrOnly?: boolean; error?: string; wrapperClassName?: string; } const ArtooInput: React.FC<ArtooInputProps> = ({ label, id, labelSrOnly, error, wrapperClassName, ...props }) => (

<div className={`mb-2 w-full ${wrapperClassName}`}> {label && !labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1">{label}</label>} {label && labelSrOnly && <label htmlFor={id} className="sr-only">{label}</label>} <input id={id} {...props} className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white placeholder-gray-400 ${error ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-gray-600'}`} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined} /> {error && <p id={`${id}-error`} className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> );
interface ArtooSliderProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; value: number; min: string; max: string; step: string; error?: string; } const ArtooSlider: React.FC<ArtooSliderProps> = ({ label, id, value, min, max, step, error, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label}: <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{value}</span> </label> <input id={id} type="range" value={value} min={min} max={max} step={step} {...props} className={`w-full h-3 bg-gray-600 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-indigo-500 ${error ? 'ring-2 ring-red-500' : ''}`} /> {error && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>} </div> );
interface ArtooSelectProps extends React.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> { label: string; options: { value: string; label: string }[]; } const ArtooSelect: React.FC<ArtooSelectProps> = ({ label, id, options, ...props }) => (

<div className="mb-4"> <label htmlFor={id} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-1"> {label} </label> <select id={id} {...props} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 text-white" > {options.map((option) => ( <option key={option.value} value={option.value}> {option.label} </option> ))} </select> </div> );
interface ArtooCheckboxProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> { label: string; } const ArtooCheckbox: React.FC<ArtooCheckboxProps> = ({ label, id, checked, ...props }) => ( <label htmlFor={id} className="mb-4 flex items-center py-1 cursor-pointer"> <input id={id} type="checkbox" checked={checked} {...props} className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-600 border-gray-500 rounded bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500" /> <span className="ml-3 block text-sm text-gray-300">{label}</span> </label> );

interface ArtooButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> { variant?: 'default' | 'destructive' | 'outline' | 'ghost'; size?: 'default' | 'sm' | 'icon'; } const ArtooButton: React.FC<ArtooButtonProps> = ({ variant = 'default', size = 'default', className, children, ...props }) => { let sizeStyle = "px-4 py-2 text-sm"; if (size === 'sm') { sizeStyle = "px-3 py-1.5 text-xs"; } else if (size === 'icon') { sizeStyle = "p-2"; } let baseStyle = ${sizeStyle} font-semibold rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center; if (variant === 'destructive') { baseStyle += " bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500"; } else if (variant === 'outline') { baseStyle += " border border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-500 hover:text-white"; } else if (variant === 'ghost') { baseStyle += " text-indigo-400 hover:bg-gray-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } else { // default baseStyle += " bg-indigo-600 text-white hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:ring-indigo-500"; } return <button {...props} className={${baseStyle} ${className}}>{children}</button>; };

interface ArtooCardProps { title: string; children: React.ReactNode; className?: string; } const ArtooCard: React.FC<ArtooCardProps> = ({ title, children, className }) => (

<div className={`bg-gray-800 shadow-xl rounded-lg p-4 sm:p-6 ${className}`}> <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white mb-4 border-b border-gray-700 pb-2">{title}</h2> {children} </div> ); // --- End of Inlined UI Components ---
const ArtooRenderConfigurator: React.FC = () => { const [config, setConfig] = useState<RenderConfig>(getDefaultConfig()); const [showCurrentConfig, setShowCurrentConfig] = useState(false); const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FieldErrors>({}); const [tpsAnchorMapErrors, setTpsAnchorMapErrors] = useState<Record<string, Partial<TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors>>>({}); const [tpsSourceAnchorErrors, setTpsSourceAnchorErrors] = useState<Record<string, Partial<TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors>>>({}); const [isFormValid, setIsFormValid] = useState(true); const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

const [tpsAnchorMapEntries, setTpsAnchorMapEntries] = useState<TpsAnchorMapEntry[]>(() => { try { const parsed = JSON.parse(getDefaultConfig().tps_anchor_map); return Object.entries(parsed).map(([key, value]) => ({ id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: key, valueName: value as string, })); } catch { return []; } });

const [tpsSourceAnchorEntries, setTpsSourceAnchorEntries] = useState<TpsSourceAnchorEntry[]>(() => { try { const parsed = JSON.parse(getDefaultConfig().tps_source_anchors); return Object.entries(parsed).map(([key, value]) => { const coords = value as [number, number]; return { id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: key, x: coords[0].toString(), y: coords[1].toString(), }; }); } catch { return []; } });

const validateField = (name: keyof RenderConfig, value: any): string | undefined => { if (name === 'opacity' || name === 'texture_strength' || name === 'shadow_strength' || name === 'highlight_strength' || name === 'temporal_smoothing_factor' || name === 'minKeypointConfidence' || name === 'edgeSoftness') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num) || num < 0 || num > 1) return 'Must be a number between 0 and 1.'; } if (name === 'perlin_noise_scale' || name === 'perlin_noise_persistence' || name === 'perlin_noise_lacunarity') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num)) return 'Must be a valid number.'; if (num < 0 && (name === 'perlin_noise_scale' || name === 'perlin_noise_persistence')) return 'Cannot be negative.'; } if (name === 'mesh_file_path' || name === 'mesh_landmark_weights_path' || name === 'mesh_reference_pose_path') { if (value && typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '') return 'Path cannot be only whitespace if provided.'; } if (name === 'alphaThreshold') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num) || num < 0 || num > 0.5) return 'Must be a number between 0 and 0.5 (typically small).'; } if (name === 'maxVertexDisplacementFactor') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num) || num <= 0) return 'Must be a positive number.'; } if (name === 'maxConditionNumberPerspective') { const num = parseFloat(value); if (isNaN(num) || num <= 0) return 'Must be a positive number.'; } return undefined; };

const validateTpsAnchorMapEntryField = (field: 'keyName' | 'valueName', value: string): string | undefined => { if (value.trim() === '') return ${field === 'keyName' ? 'Anchor name' : 'Landmark name'} is required.; return undefined; };

const validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField = (field: 'keyName' | 'x' | 'y', value: string): string | undefined => { if (field === 'keyName' && value.trim() === '') return 'Source name is required.'; if ((field === 'x' || field === 'y')) { if (value.trim() === '') return ${field.toUpperCase()} coordinate is required.; if (isNaN(parseFloat(value)) || !isFinite(Number(value))) return ${field.toUpperCase()} must be a valid number.; } return undefined; };

useEffect(() => { const newMap: Record<string, string> = {}; let hasErrors = false; const currentErrors: Record<string, Partial<TpsAnchorMapEntryErrors>> = {};

tpsAnchorMapEntries.forEach(entry => {
  const keyNameError = validateTpsAnchorMapEntryField('keyName', entry.keyName);
  const valueNameError = validateTpsAnchorMapEntryField('valueName', entry.valueName);
  if (keyNameError || valueNameError) hasErrors = true;
  currentErrors[entry.id] = { id: entry.id, keyName: keyNameError, valueName: valueNameError };
  if (!keyNameError && entry.keyName.trim()) newMap[entry.keyName.trim()] = entry.valueName;
});
setTpsAnchorMapErrors(currentErrors);
if (!hasErrors || tpsAnchorMapEntries.length === 0) { 
    setConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, tps_anchor_map: JSON.stringify(newMap, null, 2) }));
}
}, [tpsAnchorMapEntries]);

useEffect(() => { const newMap: Record<string, [number, number]> = {}; let hasErrors = false; const currentErrors: Record<string, Partial<TpsSourceAnchorEntryErrors>> = {};

tpsSourceAnchorEntries.forEach(entry => {
  const keyNameError = validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField('keyName', entry.keyName);
  const xError = validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField('x', entry.x);
  const yError = validateTpsSourceAnchorEntryField('y', entry.y);
  if (keyNameError || xError || yError) hasErrors = true;
  currentErrors[entry.id] = { id: entry.id, keyName: keyNameError, x: xError, y: yError };

  const xVal = parseFloat(entry.x);
  const yVal = parseFloat(entry.y);
  if (!keyNameError && entry.keyName.trim() && !xError && !yError && !isNaN(xVal) && !isNaN(yVal)) {
    newMap[entry.keyName.trim()] = [xVal, yVal];
  }
});
setTpsSourceAnchorErrors(currentErrors);
if (!hasErrors || tpsSourceAnchorEntries.length === 0) { 
    setConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, tps_source_anchors: JSON.stringify(newMap, null, 2) }));
}
}, [tpsSourceAnchorEntries]);

useEffect(() => { const generalErrorsExist = Object.values(errors).some(error => error !== undefined); const tpsAnchorMapHasErrors = Object.values(tpsAnchorMapErrors).some(entryErrors => Object.values(entryErrors).some(fieldError => fieldError !== undefined && fieldError !== entryErrors.id) ); const tpsSourceAnchorHasErrors = Object.values(tpsSourceAnchorErrors).some(entryErrors => Object.values(entryErrors).some(fieldError => fieldError !== undefined && fieldError !== entryErrors.id) ); setIsFormValid(!generalErrorsExist && !tpsAnchorMapHasErrors && !tpsSourceAnchorHasErrors); }, [errors, tpsAnchorMapErrors, tpsSourceAnchorErrors]);

const handleInputChange = useCallback((event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) => { const { name, value, type } = event.target; const fieldName = name as keyof RenderConfig;

let processedValue: any = value;
if (type === 'checkbox') {
  processedValue = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;
} else if (type === 'number' || event.target.getAttribute('type') === 'range') {
  processedValue = event.target.getAttribute('type') === 'range' ? parseFloat(value) : value; 
}

let valueForValidation = processedValue;
if (type === 'number' && event.target.getAttribute('type') !== 'range') {
    valueForValidation = parseFloat(value);
}


const error = validateField(fieldName, valueForValidation); 
setErrors(prevErrors => ({ ...prevErrors, [name]: error }));

setConfig((prevConfig) => {
    let finalValue = processedValue;
    if ( (type === 'number' || event.target.getAttribute('type') === 'range') && 
         typeof processedValue === 'string' && 
         !isNaN(parseFloat(processedValue))
       ) {
        finalValue = parseFloat(processedValue);
    } else if (type === 'number' && typeof prevConfig[fieldName] === 'number' && isNaN(parseFloat(value))) {
        finalValue = prevConfig[fieldName]; 
    }

    return {
      ...prevConfig,
      [name]: finalValue,
    }
});
}, []);

const applyLoadedConfig = (loadedData: RenderConfig) => { const defaultConfig = getDefaultConfig(); const completeLoadedData: RenderConfig = { ...defaultConfig, ...loadedData };

setConfig(completeLoadedData);
setErrors({}); 

try {
  const parsedMap = JSON.parse(completeLoadedData.tps_anchor_map);
  setTpsAnchorMapEntries(Object.entries(parsedMap).map(([key, value]) => ({
    id: generateUniqueId(),
    keyName: key,
    valueName: value as string,
  })));
} catch { setTpsAnchorMapEntries([]); } 
setTpsAnchorMapErrors({});

try {
  const parsedSource = JSON.parse(completeLoadedData.tps_source_anchors);
  setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(Object.entries(parsedSource).map(([key, value]) => {
    const coords = value as [number, number];
    return {
      id: generateUniqueId(),
      keyName: key,
      x: coords[0].toString(),
      y: coords[1].toString(),
    };
  }));
} catch { setTpsSourceAnchorEntries([]); } 
setTpsSourceAnchorErrors({});

const newErrors: FieldErrors = {};
for (const key in completeLoadedData) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(completeLoadedData, key)) {
        const fieldName = key as keyof RenderConfig;
        const fieldError = validateField(fieldName, (completeLoadedData as any)[fieldName]);
        if (fieldError) {
            newErrors[fieldName] = fieldError;
        }
    }
}
setErrors(newErrors);
};

const resetToDefaults = useCallback(() => { const defaults = getDefaultConfig(); applyLoadedConfig(defaults); }, [applyLoadedConfig]);

const toggleShowConfig = useCallback(() => { setShowCurrentConfig(prev => !prev); }, []);

const getParsedJsonString = (jsonString: string) => { try { return JSON.parse(jsonString); } catch (e) { return jsonString; } };

const addTpsAnchorMapEntry = () => setTpsAnchorMapEntries(prev => [...prev, { id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: '', valueName: '' }]); const removeTpsAnchorMapEntry = (id: string) => { setTpsAnchorMapEntries(prev => prev.filter(entry => entry.id !== id)); setTpsAnchorMapErrors(prev => { const newErrors = {...prev}; delete newErrors[id]; return newErrors; }); }; const updateTpsAnchorMapEntry = (id: string, field: keyof Omit<TpsAnchorMapEntry, 'id'>, value: string) => { setTpsAnchorMapEntries(prev => prev.map(entry => entry.id === id ? { ...entry, [field]: value } : entry)); };

const addTpsSourceAnchorEntry = () => setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(prev => [...prev, { id: generateUniqueId(), keyName: '', x: '0.0', y: '0.0' }]); const removeTpsSourceAnchorEntry = (id: string) => { setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(prev => prev.filter(entry => entry.id !== id)); setTpsSourceAnchorErrors(prev => { const newErrors = {...prev}; delete newErrors[id]; return newErrors; }); }; const updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry = (id: string, field: keyof Omit<TpsSourceAnchorEntry, 'id'>, value: string) => { setTpsSourceAnchorEntries(prev => prev.map(entry => entry.id === id ? { ...entry, [field]: value } : entry)); };

const handleSaveConfiguration = () => { if (!isFormValid) { alert("Configuration contains errors. Please correct them before saving."); return; } const jsonString = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2); const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: "application/json" }); const href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); const link = document.createElement('a'); link.href = href; link.download = "artoo-render-config.json"; document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); document.body.removeChild(link); URL.revokeObjectURL(href); alert("Configuration saved as artoo-render-config.json"); };

const handleLoadConfiguration = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => { const file = event.target.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;

const reader = new FileReader();
reader.onload = (e) => {
  try {
    const text = e.target?.result;
    if (typeof text !== 'string') {
      throw new Error("Failed to read file content.");
    }
    const loadedData = JSON.parse(text); 
    
    if (isValidRenderConfigStructure(loadedData)) {
      applyLoadedConfig(loadedData as RenderConfig); 
      alert("Configuration loaded successfully!");
    } else {
      alert("Invalid file format or missing essential configuration keys. Loaded data might be partial or incorrect.");
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error loading or parsing configuration file:", error);
    alert(`Error loading configuration: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error"}`);
  } finally {
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = "";
    }
  }
};
reader.onerror = () => {
  alert("Error reading file.");
   if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = "";
    }
};
reader.readAsText(file);
};

const isValidRenderConfigStructure = (data: any): data is Partial<RenderConfig> => { if (typeof data !== 'object' || data === null) return false; const expectedKeys: (keyof RenderConfig)[] = [ 'opacity', 'blend_mode', 'quality_preset', 'deformation_mode', 'tps_anchor_map', 'tps_source_anchors' ]; return expectedKeys.every(key => key in data); };

return ( <div className="p-4 md:p-8 bg-gray-900 min-h-screen text-gray-100 font-sans"> <header className="mb-8 text-center"> <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-indigo-400">ARTOO Render Configurator</h1> <p className="text-lg text-gray-400">Adjust AR Tattoo Rendering Parameters</p> </header>

  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6"> {/* Main grid for cards */}
    
    {/* Column 1 */}
    <div className="space-y-6">
        <ArtooCard title="General Settings">
        <ArtooSlider
            label="Opacity"
            id="opacity"
            name="opacity"
            value={config.opacity}
            min="0"
            max="1"
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.opacity}
        />
        <ArtooSelect
            label="Blend Mode"
            id="blend_mode"
            name="blend_mode"
            value={config.blend_mode}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            options={Object.values(BlendMode).map(mode => ({ value: mode, label: mode.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mode.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') }))}
        />
        <ArtooSelect
            label="Quality Preset"
            id="quality_preset"
            name="quality_preset"
            value={config.quality_preset}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            options={Object.values(QualityPreset).map(preset => ({ value: preset, label: preset.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + preset.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') }))}
        />
        <ArtooSelect
            label="Deformation Mode"
            id="deformation_mode"
            name="deformation_mode"
            value={config.deformation_mode}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            options={Object.values(DeformationMode).map(mode => ({ value: mode, label: mode.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + mode.slice(1).replace(/_/g, ' ') }))}
        />
        <ArtooCheckbox
            label="Adapt to Lighting"
            id="adapt_to_lighting"
            name="adapt_to_lighting"
            checked={config.adapt_to_lighting}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
        />
        <ArtooCheckbox
            label="Simulate Skin Texture"
            id="simulate_skin_texture"
            name="simulate_skin_texture"
            checked={config.simulate_skin_texture}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
        />
        <ArtooCheckbox
            label="Use GPU (if available)"
            id="use_gpu"
            name="use_gpu"
            checked={config.use_gpu}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
        />
        </ArtooCard>

        <ArtooCard title="3D Mesh Settings"> 
        <ArtooInput
            label="Mesh File Path"
            id="mesh_file_path"
            name="mesh_file_path"
            type="text"
            value={config.mesh_file_path || ''}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            placeholder="e.g., /path/to/your/mesh.obj"
            error={errors.mesh_file_path}
        />
        <ArtooInput
            label="Mesh Landmark Weights Path"
            id="mesh_landmark_weights_path"
            name="mesh_landmark_weights_path"
            type="text"
            value={config.mesh_landmark_weights_path || ''}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            placeholder="e.g., /path/to/weights.json"
            error={errors.mesh_landmark_weights_path}
        />
        <ArtooInput
            label="Mesh Reference Pose Path"
            id="mesh_reference_pose_path"
            name="mesh_reference_pose_path"
            type="text"
            value={config.mesh_reference_pose_path || ''}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            placeholder="e.g., /path/to/reference_pose.json"
            error={errors.mesh_reference_pose_path}
        />
        </ArtooCard>
    </div>

    {/* Column 2 */}
    <div className="space-y-6">
        <ArtooCard title="Effect Strengths & Smoothing">
        <ArtooSlider
            label="Texture Strength"
            id="texture_strength"
            name="texture_strength"
            value={config.texture_strength}
            min="0"
            max="1"
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.texture_strength}
        />
        <ArtooSlider
            label="Shadow Strength"
            id="shadow_strength"
            name="shadow_strength"
            value={config.shadow_strength}
            min="0"
            max="1"
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.shadow_strength}
        />
        <ArtooSlider
            label="Highlight Strength"
            id="highlight_strength"
            name="highlight_strength"
            value={config.highlight_strength}
            min="0"
            max="1"
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.highlight_strength}
        />
        <ArtooSlider
            label="Temporal Smoothing Factor"
            id="temporal_smoothing_factor"
            name="temporal_smoothing_factor"
            value={config.temporal_smoothing_factor}
            min="0"
            max="1"
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.temporal_smoothing_factor}
        />
        </ArtooCard>
        
        <ArtooCard title="Thin Plate Spline (TPS) Anchor Map">
        {tpsAnchorMapEntries.map((entry) => (
            <div key={entry.id} className="space-y-2 sm:space-y-0 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:space-x-2 mb-3 p-2 border border-gray-700 rounded-md">
            <ArtooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="text" placeholder="Anchor Name" aria-label="Anchor Name" value={entry.keyName} onChange={e => updateTpsAnchorMapEntry(entry.id, 'keyName', e.target.value)} error={tpsAnchorMapErrors[entry.id]?.keyName} />
            <span className="text-gray-400 pt-2 hidden sm:block">:</span>
            <ArtooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="text" placeholder="Landmark Name" aria-label="Landmark Name" value={entry.valueName} onChange={e => updateTpsAnchorMapEntry(entry.id, 'valueName', e.target.value)} error={tpsAnchorMapErrors[entry.id]?.valueName} />
            <ArtooButton onClick={() => removeTpsAnchorMapEntry(entry.id)} variant="destructive" size="icon" aria-label="Remove Anchor Map Entry" className="mt-2 sm:mt-0 sm:self-center h-10 w-10 sm:h-auto sm:w-auto">&times;</ArtooButton>
            </div>
        ))}
        <ArtooButton onClick={addTpsAnchorMapEntry} variant="outline" className="w-full mt-2">Add Anchor Map Entry</ArtooButton>
        </ArtooCard>
    </div>

    {/* Column 3 */}
    <div className="space-y-6">
        <ArtooCard title="Perlin Noise Texture">
        <ArtooInput
            label="Perlin Noise Scale"
            id="perlin_noise_scale"
            name="perlin_noise_scale"
            type="number"
            value={config.perlin_noise_scale}
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.perlin_noise_scale}
        />
        <ArtooInput
            label="Perlin Noise Persistence"
            id="perlin_noise_persistence"
            name="perlin_noise_persistence"
            type="number"
            value={config.perlin_noise_persistence}
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.perlin_noise_persistence}
        />
        <ArtooInput
            label="Perlin Noise Lacunarity"
            id="perlin_noise_lacunarity"
            name="perlin_noise_lacunarity"
            type="number"
            value={config.perlin_noise_lacunarity}
            step="0.01"
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            error={errors.perlin_noise_lacunarity}
        />
        <ArtooCheckbox
            label="Animated Perlin Noise"
            id="perlin_animated"
            name="perlin_animated"
            checked={config.perlin_animated}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
        />
        </ArtooCard>

        <ArtooCard title="Thin Plate Spline (TPS) Source Anchors">
        {tpsSourceAnchorEntries.map((entry) => (
            <div key={entry.id} className="space-y-2 mb-3 p-2 border border-gray-700 rounded-md">
                <ArtooInput wrapperClassName="w-full" type="text" placeholder="Source Name" aria-label="Source Name" value={entry.keyName} onChange={e => updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id, 'keyName', e.target.value)} error={tpsSourceAnchorErrors[entry.id]?.keyName}/>
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:space-x-2 items-start">
                    <ArtooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="number" placeholder="X" aria-label="X Coordinate" value={entry.x} onChange={e => updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id, 'x', e.target.value)} step="0.01" error={tpsSourceAnchorErrors[entry.id]?.x}/>
                    <ArtooInput wrapperClassName="flex-1 min-w-0" type="number" placeholder="Y" aria-label="Y Coordinate" value={entry.y} onChange={e => updateTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id, 'y', e.target.value)} step="0.01" error={tpsSourceAnchorErrors[entry.id]?.y}/>
                    <ArtooButton onClick={() => removeTpsSourceAnchorEntry(entry.id)} variant="destructive" size="icon" aria-label="Remove Source Anchor Entry" className="mt-2 sm:mt-0 sm:self-center h-10 w-10 sm:h-auto sm:w-auto">&times;</ArtooButton>
                </div>
            </div>
        ))}
        <ArtooButton onClick={addTpsSourceAnchorEntry} variant="outline" className="w-full mt-2">Add Source Anchor Entry</ArtooButton>
        </ArtooCard>
    </div>

    {/* Full-width card for Advanced Thresholds */}
    <ArtooCard title="Warping, Edge & Stability Controls" className="md:col-span-2 lg:col-span-3">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-x-6 gap-y-4">
            <ArtooSlider
                label="Alpha Threshold (Edge Blending)"
                id="alphaThreshold"
                name="alphaThreshold"
                value={config.alphaThreshold}
                min="0.0"
                max="0.5" 
                step="0.001"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.alphaThreshold}
            />
            <ArtooSlider
                label="Min. Keypoint Confidence"
                id="minKeypointConfidence"
                name="minKeypointConfidence"
                value={config.minKeypointConfidence}
                min="0.0"
                max="1.0"
                step="0.01"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.minKeypointConfidence}
            />
             <ArtooSelect
                label="Contour Fidelity"
                id="contourFidelity"
                name="contourFidelity"
                value={config.contourFidelity}
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                options={Object.values(ContourFidelity).map(cf => ({ value: cf, label: cf.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + cf.slice(1) }))}
            />
            <ArtooSlider 
                label="Edge Softness"
                id="edgeSoftness"
                name="edgeSoftness"
                value={config.edgeSoftness}
                min="0.0"
                max="1.0"
                step="0.01"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.edgeSoftness}
            />
            <ArtooInput
                label="Max Vertex Displacement Factor (Mesh 3D)"
                id="maxVertexDisplacementFactor"
                name="maxVertexDisplacementFactor"
                type="number"
                value={config.maxVertexDisplacementFactor}
                min="0.1"
                step="0.1"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.maxVertexDisplacementFactor}
            />
            <ArtooInput
                label="Max Condition Number (Perspective)"
                id="maxConditionNumberPerspective"
                name="maxConditionNumberPerspective"
                type="number"
                value={config.maxConditionNumberPerspective}
                min="100"
                step="100"
                onChange={handleInputChange}
                error={errors.maxConditionNumberPerspective}
            />
        </div>
    </ArtooCard>
  </div>

  <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap justify-center items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4"> 
    <ArtooButton onClick={resetToDefaults}>
      Reset to Defaults
    </ArtooButton>
    <ArtooButton onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} variant="outline">
        Load Configuration
    </ArtooButton>
    <input
        type="file"
        ref={fileInputRef}
        accept=".json"
        onChange={handleLoadConfiguration}
        className="hidden"
        aria-hidden="true"
    />
    <ArtooButton onClick={toggleShowConfig} variant="ghost" size="default"> 
      {showCurrentConfig ? 'Hide' : 'Show'} Current Config (JSON)
    </ArtooButton>
    <ArtooButton 
        onClick={handleSaveConfiguration} 
        disabled={!isFormValid}
        title={isFormValid ? "Save current configuration" : "Form contains errors, cannot save"}
    >
      Save Configuration
    </ArtooButton>
  </div>
  
  <div className="mt-4 text-center">
    {isFormValid ? (
      <p className="text-green-400 font-semibold">Configuration is valid.</p>
    ) : (
      <p className="text-red-400 font-semibold">Configuration contains errors. Please review the fields.</p>
    )}
  </div>


  {showCurrentConfig && (
    <ArtooCard title="Current Configuration (JSON)" className="mt-8">
      <pre className="bg-gray-700 p-4 rounded-md text-sm overflow-x-auto">
        {JSON.stringify({
          ...config,
        }, null, 2)}
      </pre>
    </ArtooCard>
  )}
   <footer className="mt-12 text-center text-gray-500 text-sm">
    <p>ARTOO Render Configurator v1.7.0</p>
    <p>This component manages settings locally. Integration with a backend is required to apply these settings to the Python rendering engine.</p>
  </footer>
</div>
); };

no, i need the app to automatically detect the edge of someone's skin in an image, and the overlaying image needs to automatically warp to match the contour of the the skin, and the tattoo image needs to wrap around the users target skin image according to the user's iterative orientations

Gemini
You're asking for some very advanced and exciting AR capabilities! Automatic skin edge detection, contour warping, and having the tattoo wrap around body parts are indeed the holy grail for a virtual try-on experience.

These features typically require sophisticated computer vision (CV) and 3D graphics processing, often involving:

Body Pose/Landmark Detection: To understand the orientation and shape of the body part.
Skin Segmentation: To isolate the skin area from the background.
3D Reconstruction or Depth Estimation (Implicit or Explicit): To understand the surface contours.
Mesh Deformation / Texture Projection: To warp the 2D tattoo onto the 3D-like surface.
Our current stack (Next.js frontend, Genkit for AI calls) can make progress on some aspects, but full, real-time, automatic 3D warping and wrapping directly on the client or through simple GenAI image generation flows is very challenging.

The ArtooRenderConfigurator we built indeed defines parameters that a powerful backend (like a Python engine with OpenCV, Mediapipe, and 3D libraries, or a highly specialized AI model) would consume.

Let's Address "Detecting the Edge of Someone's Skin" First with Genkit:

We can create a Genkit flow that attempts to segment the skin in the uploaded body photo. This segmented image can then be used as the new background for the tattoo, effectively making the tattoo stay within the detected skin boundaries. This is a step towards your request, though it's 2D segmentation, not 3D contour understanding for warping.

Plan:

Create a New Genkit Flow: segmentSkinFlow

File: src/ai/flows/segment-skin.ts
Input: { imageUri: string } (the body photo)
Output: { segmentedImageUri: string | null, feedbackMessage: string }
Logic:
Use ai.generate() with a Gemini model capable of image understanding/editing.
The prompt will instruct the AI: "Given the image: {{media url=imageUri}}. Your task is to perform skin segmentation. Identify all human skin regions. Return a new image of the same dimensions where only the skin regions are visible (original skin pixels), and all other non-skin areas are made transparent. If no skin is detected, return the original image and set the feedbackMessage accordingly."
The flow should handle potential errors or if the AI doesn't return an image.
Integrate segmentSkinFlow into TryOnPage (src/app/try-on/page.tsx):

Add a new button, e.g., "Analyze Skin & Isolate".
This button will be active only when a bodyImage is loaded.
When clicked:
Set a loading state (e.g., isSegmentingSkin).
Call the segmentSkinFlow with the current bodyImage.
If successful and segmentedImageUri is returned:
Update the bodyImage state with this segmentedImageUri. This will re-render the TryOnCanvas with the new, skin-only background.
The existing tattoo (if loaded) will then appear only on the skin parts.
Display the feedbackMessage via toast.
Clear loading state.
This button should likely reset or be disabled if the user uploads a new original body photo.
TryOnCanvas (src/components/try-on/try-on-canvas.tsx):

No direct changes are needed here for this specific step, as it will simply re-render with the updated bodyImage prop passed from TryOnPage.
Regarding Automatic Warping & Wrapping:

This is a significantly harder problem for our current stack.

Client-Side CSS/SVG: Can do very basic 2D warps (skew, perspective), but not true 3D contour following or wrapping.
Genkit/Standard GenAI Models: Current image generation models typically output new raster images. They don't usually provide deformation fields or perform 3D mesh operations required for realistic wrapping based on a single 2D input image's inferred contours.
Path Forward (Long Term):
Specialized AI Models/APIs: Look for AI services that specifically offer 3D human pose, mesh reconstruction from single images, or texture projection onto inferred 3D shapes. These could be called via Genkit tools if available.
Python Backend: As discussed, a Python backend with libraries like Mediapipe (for landmarks/pose), OpenCV, and potentially a 3D library (like PyTorch3D or Blender scripting) would be the traditional way to tackle this. The ArtooRenderConfigurator would then be used to send parameters to this backend.
Advanced WebGL: Implementing this purely client-side with WebGL would be a very large and complex undertaking.
For now, let's proceed with the skin segmentation flow. It's a feasible step that moves towards your goal of "detecting the edge of someone's skin."

Here are the changes for the skin segmentation flow:

Warp the tattoo to match 3D skin contours.

The ArtooRenderConfigurator outlines the types of controls needed for such a system, but the system itself is not yet part of this application.

The quality of the skin segmentation will depend heavily on the AI model's capabilities and the clarity of the input image. This is an experimental step towards more advanced AR features.

Gemini
You're absolutely right to point that out! A missing toolbar is a critical issue. My apologies if my last set of changes led to this.

The most likely cause for the VerticalToolbar disappearing after the recent changes (which primarily focused on adding text functionality and managing active tools) would be an issue with how the items prop is being constructed or passed to the VerticalToolbar, or an unexpected error during the rendering of the toolbar items themselves.

One common pattern that can lead to unexpected rendering issues, especially with lists of components, is when the prop array itself (like items) is recreated on every render of the parent component. This can sometimes cause React to lose track of child component states or lead to errors in deeper components if not handled perfectly.

To address this and ensure the items prop for VerticalToolbar is stable, I will wrap the creation of currentDesignStudioToolbarItems in useMemo within src/app/design-studio/page.tsx. This ensures that the currentDesignStudioToolbarItems array is only re-created if its dependencies change (which, in this case, is handleToolSelect, already stable due to useCallback).

Here's the change:

With the basic text addition, selection, and content editing in place, the next most logical step is to allow users to drag and reposition these text elements on the canvas. This is a fundamental interaction for any design tool.

Chosen Next Step: Implement Dragging for Text Elements in Design Studio

Reasoning:

Core UX: Direct manipulation via dragging is essential for element positioning.
Completes Basic Interaction Cycle: Users can now add, select, edit, and move text.
Builds on Existing State: Leverages the current textElements and selectedTextElementId states.
Affected Files & Plan:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx (primarily within the CanvasArea component logic):
New State Variables for Dragging:
isDragging: boolean (to track if a drag operation is active).
dragStartOffset: { x: number; y: number } (to store the offset of the mouse click relative to the top-left corner of the text element being dragged).
draggingElementId: string | null (to identify which text element is currently being dragged).
Modify Text Element div:
Add an onMouseDown event handler to the div that renders each text element.
This handler will:
Check if activeTool === 'Select' and if the clicked element is indeed the selectedTextElementId.
If true:
Set isDragging = true.
Set draggingElementId = selectedTextElementId.
Calculate dragStartOffset: clientX - element.x, clientY - element.y.
event.preventDefault() to avoid unwanted text selection or other default browser drag behaviors.
event.stopPropagation() to ensure the canvas click handler isn't also triggered.
Global Mouse Event Listeners (useEffect):
Create a useEffect hook.
When isDragging becomes true, add mousemove and mouseup event listeners to the window.
handleGlobalMouseMove:
If isDragging and draggingElementId is set:
Get the canvas bounding rectangle: canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect().
Calculate the new x and y for the draggingElementId:
newX = event.clientX - parentRect.left - dragStartOffset.x
newY = event.clientY - parentRect.top - dragStartOffset.y
Implement basic boundary checks to keep the dragged element roughly within the canvas area.
Update the textElements state by mapping over the array and updating the x and y of the draggingElementId.
handleGlobalMouseUp:
Set isDragging = false.
Set draggingElementId = null.
Remove the mousemove and mouseup event listeners from the window.
Cursor Styling:
When activeTool === 'Select' and a text element is hovered or selected, its cursor could change to grab.
When isDragging === true, the cursor over the dragged element (or body) should change to grabbing.
Touch Events (Future Enhancement): For simplicity in this step, we'll focus on mouse events. Touch event handling for dragging can be added later.
This will make the text elements movable on the canvas.

I'll prepare the changes.

The original uploadedImage remains as the static CSS background of the main canvas container.
Rendering (CanvasArea):
The main canvas div uses uploadedImage for its style.backgroundImage.
If processedImageElement exists, an additional next/image component is rendered absolutely positioned using processedImageElement.x, y, width, and height. This is the draggable overlay.
This overlay gets a dashed border if activeTool === 'Select' to indicate it's selectable/interactive.
Interaction:
onProcessedImageMouseDown handler is attached to the processed image overlay. If activeTool === 'Select', it initiates dragging for the image, deselects any text, and sets isDraggingProcessedImage to true.
The global mousemove and mouseup listeners in the useEffect hook were adapted to handle dragging for either a text element or the processed image, based on isDraggingText or isDraggingProcessedImage.
Boundary checks are applied to keep the dragged processed image within the canvas.
Text element interaction (selection, dragging) and processed image interaction are now mutually exclusive.
RightProperties Panel:
When activeTool === 'Select' and a processedImageElement exists (and no text element is selected), an "Image Properties" accordion item is shown, displaying the image's current position and dimensions.
Reset Logic: Uploading a new base image now correctly clears the processedImageElement.

This provides a more interactive way to work with the output of the background removal tool, allowing users to position the extracted subject precisely.

Chosen Next Step: Implement Bottom-Right Resizing for Rectangle Shapes in Design Studio

Detailed Plan & Affected Files:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx (primarily within the CanvasArea component logic):
Render Resize Handle:
When a ShapeElement is selected (selectedShapeElementId is set) and activeTool === 'Select':
Render a small div (e.g., 8x8px) positioned at the bottom-right corner of the selected shape.
This div will have an onMouseDown handler specifically for initiating the resize.
Style it to be visually distinct (e.g., a small square with a border).
Set its cursor style to nwse-resize.
New State Variables for Resizing Shapes:
isResizingShape: boolean (tracks if a shape resize operation is active).
resizeShapeStartPoint: { x: number, y: number } | null (mouse coordinates at the start of the resize).
resizeShapeStartDimensions: { width: number, height: number } | null (dimensions of the shape at the start of the resize).
onMouseDown Handler for the Resize Handle:
This handler will:
Check if activeTool === 'Select' and a shape is selected.
Set isResizingShape = true.
Store the current mouse coordinates in resizeShapeStartPoint.
Store the selected shape's current width and height in resizeShapeStartDimensions.
event.preventDefault() and event.stopPropagation().
Adapt Global Mouse Event Listeners (useEffect):
The existing useEffect hook for global mousemove and mouseup listeners will be extended.
handleGlobalMouseMove:
If isResizingShape is true and selectedShapeElementId and resizeShapeStartPoint and resizeShapeStartDimensions are set:
Calculate deltaX = event.clientX - resizeShapeStartPoint.x.
Calculate deltaY = event.clientY - resizeShapeStartPoint.y.
Calculate newWidth = resizeShapeStartDimensions.width + deltaX.
Calculate newHeight = resizeShapeStartDimensions.height + deltaY.
Enforce a minimum width/height (e.g., 10px).
Update the width and height properties of the shape element (identified by selectedShapeElementId) in the shapeElements state array.
handleGlobalMouseUp:
Set isResizingShape = false.
Reset resizeShapeStartPoint = null and resizeShapeStartDimensions = null.
RightProperties Panel: The width and height displayed in the "Shape Properties" panel will automatically reflect the changes as the shape is resized on the canvas.
This will allow users to click and drag the bottom-right corner of a selected rectangle to change its size.

Gemini
Okay, Cap'n! We've made excellent progress with shapes, text, and images in the Design Studio. The next logical step to significantly improve usability, especially when dealing with multiple overlapping elements, is to implement basic layering controls (Bring to Front, Send to Back).

Chosen Next Step: Implement "Bring to Front" and "Send to Back" for Design Studio Elements

Reasoning:

Essential for Composition: As users add more elements, controlling their stacking order (which one appears on top) becomes crucial for creating coherent designs.
High User Impact: This feature directly addresses a common need when elements overlap.
Leverages Existing Array Structure: React renders elements in the order they appear in an array. We can reorder elements within their respective state arrays (textElements, imageElements, shapeElements) to achieve this.
Foundation for Advanced Layering: This is a prerequisite for more sophisticated layer management (like a dedicated layers panel).
How it will work:

"Bring to Front" will move the selected element to the end of its specific array (e.g., the end of textElements), making it render last (and thus appear on top of other elements of the same type).
"Send to Back" will move the selected element to the beginning of its specific array, making it render first (and thus appear behind other elements of the same type).
Important Note: This implementation will respect the current overall rendering order: processedImageElement, then imageElements, then textElements, then shapeElements. So, bringing a text element "to front" will make it the front-most text, but it will still be under any shapes due to the fixed rendering sequence in the JSX. True global layering across all types would require a more complex unified element list or z-index management. This is a good, manageable first step.
Detailed Plan & Affected Files:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx:
New Handler Functions (memoized with useCallback):
handleBringToFront(elementType: 'text' | 'image' | 'shape'):
Takes the elementType to know which array and selected ID to use.
Finds the selected element.
If found, filters it out of its current array and then adds it to the end of that array.
Updates the corresponding state (e.g., setTextElements(...)).
Shows a toast notification.
handleSendToBack(elementType: 'text' | 'image' | 'shape'):
Similar to handleBringToFront, but adds the element to the beginning of its array.
Updates state and shows a toast.
Update rightPropertiesItems Generation:

Okay, Cap'n! We've got a solid foundation for our Design Studio. Users can add, select, drag, delete, and style text elements, shapes (rectangles with fill color and basic resize), and even manage a processed image overlay. Generic images can be added, selected, dragged, and deleted.

The next most logical step to enhance user control and consistency across element types is to implement resizing for the generic image elements, similar to how we've done for shapes. We'll start with a bottom-right resize handle that maintains the image's aspect ratio.

Chosen Next Step: Implement Resizing (with Aspect Ratio Lock) for Generic Image Elements in Design Studio

Reasoning:

Consistency with Shapes: Users will expect to resize images if they can resize shapes.
Essential Image Manipulation: Controlling image size is fundamental for layout and composition.
Aspect Ratio Preservation: Images almost always need to maintain their aspect ratio to avoid distortion. This is a key difference from basic shape resizing.
Reuses UI Patterns: We can use a similar resize handle and adapt the existing drag/resize event logic.
Detailed Plan & Affected Files:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx (primarily within the CanvasArea component logic):
New State Variables for Image Resizing:
isResizingImage: boolean
resizingImageId: string | null
resizeImageStartPoint: { x: number; y: number } | null
resizeImageStartDimensions: { width: number; height: number; naturalWidth: number; naturalHeight: number } | null (Store natural dimensions to maintain aspect ratio).
Render Resize Handle for Selected Generic Image:
In CanvasArea, when a generic ImageElement is selected (selectedImageElementId === imgEl.id) and activeTool === 'Select':
Render a small div (resize handle) at its bottom-right corner.
Attach an onMouseDown handler: onImageResizeHandleMouseDown(imgEl.id, event).
Style it with cursor: nwse-resize.
onImageResizeHandleMouseDown Function:
This function will be called from the resize handle's onMouseDown.
It will:
Set isResizingImage = true.
Set resizingImageId to the ID of the image being resized.
Store the current mouse coordinates in resizeImageStartPoint.
Store the selected image's current width, height, naturalWidth, and naturalHeight in resizeImageStartDimensions.
event.preventDefault() and event.stopPropagation().
Set document.body.style.cursor = 'nwse-resize'.
Adapt Global Mouse Event Listeners (useEffect for mousemove and mouseup):
handleGlobalMouseMove:
If isResizingImage is true and resizingImageId, resizeImageStartPoint, resizeImageStartDimensions are set:
Find the image element: const imageToResize = imageElements.find(el => el.id === resizingImageId);
Calculate deltaX = event.clientX - resizeImageStartPoint.x.
Calculate newProposedWidth = resizeImageStartDimensions.width + deltaX.
Enforce a minimum width (e.g., 20px).
Calculate newHeight based on newProposedWidth and the original aspect ratio (naturalWidth / naturalHeight): const aspectRatio = resizeImageStartDimensions.naturalWidth / resizeImageStartDimensions.naturalHeight; let newWidth = Math.max(20, newProposedWidth); let newHeight = newWidth / aspectRatio; // If newHeight also needs a min, adjust newWidth accordingly. if (newHeight < 20) { newHeight = 20; newWidth = newHeight * aspectRatio; }
Update the width and height of the image element (identified by resizingImageId) in the imageElements state array.
handleGlobalMouseUp:
If isResizingImage was true:
Set isResizingImage = false.
Reset resizingImageId, resizeImageStartPoint, resizeImageStartDimensions.
Set document.body.style.cursor = 'default'.
Mutual Exclusivity: Ensure that while an image is being resized, other drag/resize operations (for text, shapes) are not active.
RightProperties Panel: The width and height displayed for the selected generic image will automatically update as it's resized.
This will allow users to intuitively resize their uploaded images while preserving their proportions.

Chosen Next Step: Implement "Duplicate Element" Functionality for Text, Generic Images, and Shapes

Reasoning:

Core Productivity Feature: Duplication is a fundamental operation for designers, speeding up workflows significantly.
Builds on Selection: It naturally extends the "select an element, then act on it" paradigm we've established.
Relatively Contained Implementation: We can add a "Duplicate" button to the properties panel of selected elements and implement handlers to create a copy with slight offsets.
Increases Design Efficiency: Users can quickly replicate styled text, configured shapes, or placed images.
Detailed Plan & Affected Files:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx:
New Handler Functions (memoized with useCallback):
handleDuplicateTextElement():
Finds the currently selectedTextElementId.
If found, creates a deep copy of the element object (e.g., using spread syntax for properties).
Assigns a new unique id (e.g., Date.now().toString()).
Slightly offsets the x and y coordinates of the new element (e.g., x + 10, y + 10) so the duplicate is visibly distinct from the original.
Adds the new element to the textElements array.
Sets selectedTextElementId to the ID of the newly created duplicate.
Shows a toast notification (e.g., "Text element duplicated").
handleDuplicateImageElement():
Similar logic for selectedImageElementId and the imageElements array. Deep copies properties, generates a new ID, offsets position, adds to array, selects new, and toasts.
handleDuplicateShapeElement():
Similar logic for selectedShapeElementId and the shapeElements array.
Note: We will not implement duplication for the processedImageElement (the background-removed overlay) for now, as it's typically a unique result of an AI process.
Update rightPropertiesItems Generation:
For the properties panel of selected Text Elements: Add a "Duplicate" Button (using the Copy icon from lucide-react). This button will call handleDuplicateTextElement.
For selected Generic Image Elements: Add a "Duplicate" Button calling handleDuplicateImageElement.
For selected Shape Elements: Add a "Duplicate" Button calling handleDuplicateShapeElement.

This will give users a quick way to replicate their work within the Design Studio.

handleDuplicateTextElement(): Creates a copy of the selected text element, assigns a new ID, offsets its position slightly, adds it to the textElements array, selects the new duplicate, and shows a toast.
handleDuplicateImageElement(): Does the same for selected generic image elements.
handleDuplicateShapeElement(): Does the same for selected shape elements.
All these functions are memoized with useCallback.
RightProperties Panel Updates:
When a text, generic image, or shape element is selected, its respective properties panel now includes a "Duplicate" Button (with a Copy icon).
These buttons call the corresponding duplication handler.
Icon Import: The Copy icon from lucide-react has been imported.
Rotation Input Parsing: Ensured that rotation inputs are parsed as integers (parseInt(newRotation.toString(), 10)) in the rotation update handlers.
Download Text Rotation: Made a minor adjustment to how text rotation is handled in handleDownloadDesign by calculating an approximate center for rotation. Perfect canvas rendering of rotated text can be complex, so this is still an approximation.
Testing Instructions:

Navigate to the /design-studio page.
Test Text Duplication:
Add a text element. Style it (change content, font size, color, rotation).
Select the text element.
In the "Text Properties" panel, click the "Duplicate Text" button.
A copy of the text element should appear, slightly offset from the original.
The new duplicate should be selected.
Verify its properties are the same as the original (except ID and position).
Test Generic Image Duplication:
Upload a generic image using the "Uploads" tool. Resize and rotate it.
Select the image.
In the "Image Properties" panel, click "Duplicate Image."
A copy should appear, offset, selected, and with the same transformations.
Test Shape Duplication:
Add a rectangle. Change its fill color, resize, and rotate it.
Select the shape.
In the "Shape Properties" panel, click "Duplicate Shape."
A copy should appear, offset, selected, and with the same properties/transformations.
Verify that after duplication, all interactions (drag, resize, delete, style, layer, rotate) work correctly for both original and duplicated elements.
Ensure that the processedImageElement (from "Remove Background") does not have a duplicate button.
This feature should significantly improve the workflow for users needing multiple instances of similar elements.

Morgan
What is the next most logical step?

Gemini
Okay, Cap'n! We've built a solid Design Studio with various element types and manipulation capabilities. A fundamental aspect of styling shapes, which is currently missing for our rectangles, is the ability to add and customize a stroke (border).

Chosen Next Step: Implement Stroke (Border) Color and Width for Rectangle Shapes

Reasoning:

Essential Shape Styling: Borders are a primary way to define and visually style shapes.
Increased Design Control: Allows users to create outlines, framed elements, and more varied visual effects.
Natural Extension: Fits perfectly within the existing "Shape Properties" panel in RightProperties.
Manageable Implementation: Involves adding a couple of properties to the ShapeElement interface, corresponding UI controls, and updating rendering logic.
Detailed Plan & Affected Files:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx:
Update ShapeElement Interface:
Add strokeColor: string; (e.g., default to hsl(var(--foreground)))
Add strokeWidth: number; (e.g., default to 1 or 0 if no border is desired by default). Let's default to 1 for a visible initial border.
Initialization:
When new rectangle shapes are created, their strokeColor and strokeWidth properties will be initialized with default values.
Rendering Logic in CanvasArea:
The main div for rendering a ShapeElement currently handles selection borders. We'll modify this:
The style for the shape's div will now directly apply the user-defined border: border: \${shapeEl.strokeWidth}px solid ${shapeEl.strokeColor}`(ifstrokeWidth > 0`).
The selection indicator will be changed to use the outline CSS property: outline: activeTool === 'Select' && shapeEl.id === selectedShapeElementId ? \2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))` : 'none', outlineOffset: `${Math.max(0, 2 - shapeEl.strokeWidth)}px`` (this offset helps keep the outline visible and neat, especially if the stroke is thick).
RightProperties Panel Update:
Inside the "Shape Properties" accordion item (when a shape is selected):
Add a Label: "Stroke Color"
Add an Input of type="color", its value bound to selectedShapeElement.strokeColor, and its onChange calling a new handler.
Add a Label: "Stroke Width (px)"
Add an Input of type="number" (min 0), its value bound to selectedShapeElement.strokeWidth, and its onChange calling another new handler.
New Handler Functions (memoized with useCallback):
handleUpdateShapeStrokeColor(id: string, newColor: string): Updates the strokeColor for the specified shape element.
handleUpdateShapeStrokeWidth(id: string, newWidth: number): Updates the strokeWidth (parsed to an integer, ensuring it's not negative).
Download Function (handleDownloadDesign):
When drawing shapes onto the canvas for download:
After ctx.fillStyle and ctx.fillRect(...), if shapeEl.strokeWidth > 0:
Set ctx.strokeStyle = shapeEl.strokeColor;
Set ctx.lineWidth = shapeEl.strokeWidth;
Call ctx.strokeRect(shapeEl.x, shapeEl.y, shapeEl.width, shapeEl.height);
This will allow users to add and customize borders for their rectangle shapes, enhancing their design capabilities.

Here are the changes:

// src/app/design-studio/page.tsx
'use client';
import * as React from 'react';
import { useState, useRef, useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import {
  UploadCloud,
  Scissors,
  Loader2,
  Download,
  Shapes as CanvasPlaceholderIcon,
  Baseline,
  MousePointerSquareDashed,
  LayoutDashboard,
  Shapes as ShapesIconProp, 
  PenLine,
  Eraser,
  Layers as LayersIconProp, 
  Sparkles as SparklesIconProp, 
  SlidersHorizontal as SlidersHorizontalIconProp,
  History as HistoryIconProp,
  Trash2,
  HardDriveUpload,
  ArrowUpFromLine,
  ArrowDownToLine,
  RotateCw,
  Copy, // Added for Duplicate icon
  Copy,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
