interface TextElement {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  fontSize: number;
  color: string;
  rotation: number;
}
interface ProcessedImageElement {
  src: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  naturalWidth: number;
  naturalHeight: number;
  rotation: number;
}
interface ImageElement {
  id: string;
  src: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  naturalWidth: number;
  naturalHeight: number;
  rotation: number;
}
interface ShapeElement {
  id: string;
  type: 'rectangle';
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  fillColor: string;
  rotation: number;
  strokeColor: string;
  strokeWidth: number;
}
interface CanvasAreaProps {
  activeTool: string;
  uploadedImage: string | null;
  
  processedImageElement: ProcessedImageElement | null;
  isProcessedImageActiveForProperties: boolean; // Added this prop
  isProcessingBackground: boolean;
  
  textElements: TextElement[];
  selectedTextElementId: string | null;
  setSelectedTextElementId: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string | null>>;
  
  imageElements: ImageElement[];
  selectedImageElementId: string | null;
  setSelectedImageElementId: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string | null>>;
  shapeElements: ShapeElement[];
  selectedShapeElementId: string | null;
  setSelectedShapeElementId: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string | null>>;
  
  onImageUpload: (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;
  onRemoveBackground: () => Promise<void>;
  onDownloadDesign: () => void;
  
  onCanvasClick: (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  
  onTextElementMouseDown: (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  onProcessedImageMouseDown: (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  onGenericImageMouseDown: (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  onShapeElementMouseDown: (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  
  onShapeResizeHandleMouseDown: (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  onImageResizeHandleMouseDown: (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  onProcessedImageResizeHandleMouseDown: (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void;
  fileInputRef: React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement>;
  canvasContainerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
}
function CanvasArea({
  activeTool,
  uploadedImage,
  processedImageElement,
  isProcessedImageActiveForProperties,
  isProcessingBackground,
  textElements,
  selectedTextElementId,
  setSelectedTextElementId,
  imageElements,
  selectedImageElementId,
  setSelectedImageElementId,
  shapeElements,
  selectedShapeElementId,
  setSelectedShapeElementId,
  onImageUpload,
  onRemoveBackground,
  onDownloadDesign,
  onCanvasClick,
  onTextElementMouseDown,
  onProcessedImageMouseDown,
  onGenericImageMouseDown,
  onShapeElementMouseDown,
  onShapeResizeHandleMouseDown,
  onImageResizeHandleMouseDown,
  onProcessedImageResizeHandleMouseDown,
  fileInputRef,
  canvasContainerRef,
}: CanvasAreaProps) {
  const displayImageForBackground = uploadedImage;
  const isProcessedImageActiveForProperties = activeTool === 'Select' && processedImageElement && !selectedTextElementId && !selectedImageElementId && !selectedShapeElementId;
  return (
    <section className="flex-1 p-6 md:p-8 bg-background flex flex-col overflow-auto items-center space-y-6">
      <Card className="w-full max-w-2xl shadow-lg border-border rounded-xl">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-primary">Design Controls</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload base image, add elements, or manage your design.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <Input
            type="file"
            accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp"
            ref={fileInputRef}
            className="hidden"
            id="imageUploadInput"
            multiple={false} 
          />
          <Button
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            variant="outline"
            className="w-full py-3 text-base"
            disabled={isProcessingBackground}
          >
            <UploadCloud className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
            {uploadedImage ? "Change Base Image" : "Upload Base Image"}
          </Button>
          {uploadedImage && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <Button
                onClick={onRemoveBackground}
                className="w-full py-3 text-base bg-accent hover:bg-accent/90 text-accent-foreground"
                disabled={isProcessingBackground || !uploadedImage}
              >
                {isProcessingBackground ? (
                  <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Scissors className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                )}
                Remove Background
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                className="w-full py-3 text-base border-primary text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-primary"
                onClick={onDownloadDesign}
                aria-label="Download design"
                disabled={isProcessingBackground || (!uploadedImage && !processedImageElement && textElements.length === 0 && imageElements.length === 0 && shapeElements.length === 0)}
              >
                <Download className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                Download Design
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      {(isProcessingBackground && !processedImageElement && !uploadedImage) && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-md flex flex-col items-center text-center p-8 rounded-xl bg-card shadow-lg border-border">
          <Loader2 className="h-12 w-12 animate-spin text-primary mb-4" />
          <p className="text-lg font-semibold">Processing image background...</p>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">This might take a moment.</p>
        </div>
      )}
      <div
        ref={canvasContainerRef}
        className={cn(
            "relative w-full max-w-2xl min-h-[400px] bg-muted/30 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-border flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground p-0 overflow-hidden select-none",
             activeTool === 'Text' ? 'cursor-text' : (activeTool === 'Elements' ? 'cursor-crosshair' : (activeTool === 'Select' ? 'cursor-default' : 'cursor-crosshair'))
        )}
        onClick={onCanvasClick}
        style={{
          backgroundImage: displayImageForBackground ? `url(${displayImageForBackground})` : 'none',
          backgroundSize: 'contain',
          backgroundPosition: 'center',
          backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat',
        }}
      >
        {!displayImageForBackground && !isProcessingBackground && !processedImageElement && textElements.length === 0 && imageElements.length === 0 && shapeElements.length === 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-6 pointer-events-none select-none">
            <CanvasPlaceholderIcon className="h-20 w-20 mb-4 opacity-30" />
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Design Canvas</h2>
            <p className="text-sm">Upload a base image to begin, or use tools from the sidebar.</p>
          </div>
        )}
        {processedImageElement && (
          <div 
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              left: `${processedImageElement.x}px`,
              top: `${processedImageElement.y}px`,
              width: `${processedImageElement.width}px`,
              height: `${processedImageElement.height}px`,
              cursor: isProcessedImageActiveForProperties ? 'grab' : 'default',
              border: isProcessedImageActiveForProperties ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : '2px solid transparent',
              outline: isProcessedImageActiveForProperties ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : 'none',
              outlineOffset: '2px',
              borderRadius: '2px',
              transform: `rotate(${processedImageElement.rotation}deg)`,
              transformOrigin: 'center center',
            }}
            onMouseDown={onProcessedImageMouseDown}
          >
            <Image
              src={processedImageElement.src}
              alt="Processed Design Element"
              width={processedImageElement.naturalWidth}
              height={processedImageElement.naturalHeight}
              className="object-contain select-none w-full h-full"
              draggable={false}
            />
             {isProcessedImageActiveForProperties && (
              <div 
                className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-3 h-3 bg-card border border-primary rounded-full cursor-nwse-resize"
                style={{ zIndex: 10 }}
                onMouseDown={onProcessedImageResizeHandleMouseDown}
              />
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        {imageElements.map(imgEl => (
           <div 
            key={imgEl.id}
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              left: `${imgEl.x}px`,
              top: `${imgEl.y}px`,
              width: `${imgEl.width}px`,
              height: `${imgEl.height}px`,
              cursor: activeTool === 'Select' && imgEl.id === selectedImageElementId ? 'grab' : (activeTool === 'Select' ? 'pointer' : 'default'),
              border: activeTool === 'Select' && imgEl.id === selectedImageElementId ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : '2px solid transparent',
              outline: activeTool === 'Select' && imgEl.id === selectedImageElementId ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : 'none',
              outlineOffset: '2px',
              borderRadius: '2px',
              transform: `rotate(${imgEl.rotation}deg)`,
              transformOrigin: 'center center',
            }}
            onMouseDown={(e) => onGenericImageMouseDown(imgEl.id, e)}
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <Image
              src={imgEl.src}
              alt={`Uploaded element ${imgEl.id}`}
              width={imgEl.naturalWidth}
              height={imgEl.naturalHeight}
              className="object-contain select-none w-full h-full"
              draggable={false}
            />
            {activeTool === 'Select' && imgEl.id === selectedImageElementId && (
              <div 
                className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-3 h-3 bg-card border border-primary rounded-full cursor-nwse-resize"
                style={{ zIndex: 10 }}
                onMouseDown={(e) => onImageResizeHandleMouseDown(imgEl.id, e)}
              />
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
        {textElements.map(textEl => (
          <div
            key={textEl.id}
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              left: `${textEl.x}px`,
              top: `${textEl.y}px`,
              cursor: activeTool === 'Select' && textEl.id === selectedTextElementId ? 'grab' : (activeTool === 'Select' ? 'pointer' : 'default'),
              color: textEl.color,
              fontSize: `${textEl.fontSize}px`,
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              userSelect: 'none',
              padding: '4px',
              border: activeTool === 'Select' && textEl.id === selectedTextElementId ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : '2px solid transparent',
              outline: activeTool === 'Select' && textEl.id === selectedTextElementId ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : 'none',
              outlineOffset: '2px',
              borderRadius: '4px',
              transform: `rotate(${textEl.rotation}deg)`,
              transformOrigin: 'center center',
            }}
            onClick={(e) => { 
                 e.stopPropagation();
                 if (activeTool === 'Select') {
                    setSelectedTextElementId(textEl.id);
                    setSelectedImageElementId(null);
                    setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
                 }
            }}
            onMouseDown={(e) => onTextElementMouseDown(textEl.id, e)}
          >
            {textEl.content}
          </div>
        ))}
        
        {shapeElements.map(shapeEl => (
          <div 
            key={shapeEl.id}
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              left: `${shapeEl.x}px`,
              top: `${shapeEl.y}px`,
              width: `${shapeEl.width}px`,
              height: `${shapeEl.height}px`,
              cursor: activeTool === 'Select' && shapeEl.id === selectedShapeElementId ? 'grab' : (activeTool === 'Select' ? 'pointer' : 'default'),
              border: activeTool === 'Select' && shapeEl.id === selectedShapeElementId ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : '2px solid transparent',
              borderRadius: shapeEl.type === 'rectangle' ? '2px' : '50%',
              border: shapeEl.strokeWidth > 0 ? `${shapeEl.strokeWidth}px solid ${shapeEl.strokeColor}` : 'none',
              outline: activeTool === 'Select' && shapeEl.id === selectedShapeElementId ? '2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))' : 'none',
              outlineOffset: `${Math.max(0, 2 - shapeEl.strokeWidth)}px`,
              borderRadius: shapeEl.type === 'rectangle' ? '2px' : '50%', // Only for visual on canvas, not for shape type logic itself
              transform: `rotate(${shapeEl.rotation}deg)`,
              transformOrigin: 'center center',
            }}
            onClick={(e) => {
                 e.stopPropagation();
                 if (activeTool === 'Select') {
                    setSelectedShapeElementId(shapeEl.id);
                    setSelectedTextElementId(null);
                    setSelectedImageElementId(null);
                 }
            }}
            onMouseDown={(e) => onShapeElementMouseDown(shapeEl.id, e)}
          >
            <div 
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                height: '100%',
                backgroundColor: shapeEl.fillColor,
                borderRadius: shapeEl.type === 'rectangle' ? '1px' : '50%', 
                borderRadius: shapeEl.type === 'rectangle' ? '1px' : '50%', // Inner visual consistency
              }}
            />
            {activeTool === 'Select' && shapeEl.id === selectedShapeElementId && (
              <div 
                className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-3 h-3 bg-card border border-primary rounded-full cursor-nwse-resize"
                style={{ zIndex: 10 }}
                onMouseDown={(e) => onShapeResizeHandleMouseDown(shapeEl.id, e)}
              />
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
export default function DesignStudioPage() {
  const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<string>('Select');
  const [uploadedImage, setUploadedImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const [processedImageElement, setProcessedImageElement] = useState<ProcessedImageElement | null>(null);
  const [isProcessingBackground, setIsProcessingBackground] = useState(false);
  
  const [textElements, setTextElements] = useState<TextElement[]>([]);
  const [selectedTextElementId, setSelectedTextElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [imageElements, setImageElements] = useState<ImageElement[]>([]);
  const [selectedImageElementId, setSelectedImageElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shapeElements, setShapeElements] = useState<ShapeElement[]>([]);
  const [selectedShapeElementId, setSelectedShapeElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDraggingText, setIsDraggingText] = useState(false);
  const [dragStartOffsetText, setDragStartOffsetText] = useState<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [draggingTextElementId, setDraggingTextElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDraggingProcessedImage, setIsDraggingProcessedImage] = useState(false);
  const [dragStartOffsetProcessedImage, setDragStartOffsetProcessedImage] = useState<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isDraggingGenericImage, setIsDraggingGenericImage] = useState(false);
  const [draggingGenericImageId, setDraggingGenericImageId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dragStartOffsetGenericImage, setDragStartOffsetGenericImage] = useState<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isDraggingShapeElement, setIsDraggingShapeElement] = useState(false);
  const [draggingShapeElementId, setDraggingShapeElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dragStartOffsetShapeElement, setDragStartOffsetShapeElement] = useState<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isResizingShape, setIsResizingShape] = useState(false);
  const [resizeShapeStartPoint, setResizeShapeStartPoint] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [resizeShapeStartDimensions, setResizeShapeStartDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number } | null>(null);
  const [isResizingImage, setIsResizingImage] = useState(false);
  const [resizingImageId, setResizingImageId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resizeImageStartPoint, setResizeImageStartPoint] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [resizeImageStartDimensions, setResizeImageStartDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number; naturalWidth: number; naturalHeight: number } | null>(null);
  const [isResizingProcessedImage, setIsResizingProcessedImage] = useState(false);
  const [resizeProcessedImageStartPoint, setResizeProcessedImageStartPoint] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [resizeProcessedImageStartDimensions, setResizeProcessedImageStartDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number; naturalWidth: number; naturalHeight: number } | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const canvasContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  useEffect(() => {
 useEffect(() => {
    setUploadedImage(null);
    setProcessedImageElement(null);
    setIsProcessingBackground(false);
    setTextElements([]);
    setSelectedTextElementId(null);
    setImageElements([]);
    setSelectedImageElementId(null);
    setShapeElements([]);
    setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
    setActiveTool('Select');
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  }, []);
  const handleToolSelect = useCallback((toolLabel: string) => {
    setActiveTool(toolLabel);
    if (toolLabel !== 'Select') {
      setSelectedTextElementId(null);
      setSelectedImageElementId(null);
      setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
    }
    if (toolLabel === 'Uploads' && fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.onchange = handleElementImageUpload; // Ensure correct handler for "Uploads"
      fileInputRef.current.onchange = handleElementImageUpload; 
      fileInputRef.current.click();
    } else if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.onchange = handleBaseImageUpload; // Default to base image upload
      fileInputRef.current.onchange = handleBaseImageUpload; 
    }
  }, []);
  }, [ ]); 
  const handleBaseImageUpload = useCallback((event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "File Too Large", description: "Please upload an image smaller than 5MB." });
        if (event.target) event.target.value = '';
        return;
      }
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onloadend = () => {
        setUploadedImage(reader.result as string);
        setProcessedImageElement(null); 
        setTextElements([]);
        setSelectedTextElementId(null);
        setImageElements([]);
        setSelectedImageElementId(null);
        setShapeElements([]);
        setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
        toast({ title: "Base Image Uploaded", description: "Your image is set as the canvas background." });
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
     if (event.target) event.target.value = '';
  }, [toast]);
  
  const handleElementImageUpload = useCallback((event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (file && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
        toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "File Too Large", description: "Max 5MB." });
        if (event.target) event.target.value = '';
        return;
      }
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onloadend = () => {
        const newImg = new window.Image();
        newImg.onload = () => {
          const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
          const MAX_DIM = 200;
          let w = newImg.naturalWidth;
          let h = newImg.naturalHeight;
          const aspectRatio = w / h;
          if (w > MAX_DIM || h > MAX_DIM) {
            if (aspectRatio > 1) { 
              w = MAX_DIM;
              h = MAX_DIM / aspectRatio;
            } else { 
              h = MAX_DIM;
              w = MAX_DIM * aspectRatio;
            }
          }
          const newImageElement: ImageElement = {
            id: Date.now().toString(),
            src: newImg.src,
            x: (canvasRect.width - w) / 2,
            y: (canvasRect.height - h) / 2,
            width: w,
            height: h,
            naturalWidth: newImg.naturalWidth,
            naturalHeight: newImg.naturalHeight,
            rotation: 0,
          };
          setImageElements(prev => [...prev, newImageElement]);
          setSelectedImageElementId(newImageElement.id);
          setSelectedTextElementId(null);
          setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
          setActiveTool('Select');
          toast({ title: "Image Element Added" });
        };
        newImg.src = reader.result as string;
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
    if (event.target) {
      event.target.value = ''; 
    }
  }, [toast]);
  const handleRemoveBackground = async () => {
    if (!uploadedImage) {
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "No Base Image", description: "Please upload a base image first." });
      return;
    }
    setIsProcessingBackground(true);
    setSelectedTextElementId(null); 
    setSelectedImageElementId(null);
    setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
    try {
      const result = await removeBackground({ imageDataUri: uploadedImage });
      if (result.processedImageDataUri) {
        const img = new window.Image();
        img.onload = () => {
          const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
          const canvasWidth = canvasRect?.width || img.naturalWidth;
          const canvasHeight = canvasRect?.height || img.naturalHeight;
          
          let displayWidth = img.naturalWidth;
          let displayHeight = img.naturalHeight;
          const maxDim = Math.min(canvasWidth * 0.8, canvasHeight * 0.8); 
          if (displayWidth > maxDim || displayHeight > maxDim) {
            const aspectRatio = displayWidth / displayHeight;
            if (aspectRatio > 1) { 
              displayWidth = maxDim;
              displayHeight = maxDim / aspectRatio;
            } else { 
              displayHeight = maxDim;
              displayWidth = maxDim * aspectRatio;
            }
          }
          setProcessedImageElement({
            src: result.processedImageDataUri,
            x: (canvasWidth - displayWidth) / 2,
            y: (canvasHeight - displayHeight) / 2,
            width: displayWidth,
            height: displayHeight,
            naturalWidth: img.naturalWidth,
            naturalHeight: img.naturalHeight,
            rotation: 0,
          });
          setActiveTool('Select'); 
          toast({ title: "Background Removed!", description: "The processed image is now an overlay." });
        };
        img.onerror = () => {
          toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Image Load Error", description: "Could not load processed image dimensions."});
        }
        img.src = result.processedImageDataUri;
      } else {
        throw new Error("AI did not return a processed image.");
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error removing background:", error);
      toast({ variant: "destructive", title: "Processing Failed", description: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to remove background." });
    } finally {
      setIsProcessingBackground(false);
    }
  };
  const handleDownloadDesign = () => {
    const imageToDownload = processedImageElement?.src || uploadedImage;
    if (!imageToDownload && textElements.length === 0 && imageElements.length === 0 && shapeElements.length === 0) {
      toast({variant: "destructive", title: "Nothing to Download", description: "Upload an image or create a design first."});
      return;
    }
    
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx || !canvasContainerRef.current) {
        toast({variant: "destructive", title: "Download Error", description: "Could not prepare download."});
        return;
    }
    const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    canvas.width = canvasRect.width;
    canvas.height = canvasRect.height;
    const drawPromises: Promise<void>[] = [];
    if (uploadedImage) {
        const baseImg = new window.Image();
        baseImg.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; 
        drawPromises.push(new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            baseImg.onload = () => {
                const hRatio = canvas.width / baseImg.naturalWidth;
                const vRatio = canvas.height / baseImg.naturalHeight;
                const ratio  = Math.min(hRatio, vRatio);
                const centerShift_x = (canvas.width - baseImg.naturalWidth * ratio) / 2;
                const centerShift_y = (canvas.height - baseImg.naturalHeight * ratio) / 2;  
                ctx.drawImage(baseImg, 0, 0, baseImg.naturalWidth, baseImg.naturalHeight,
                              centerShift_x, centerShift_y, baseImg.naturalWidth * ratio, baseImg.naturalHeight * ratio);
                resolve();
            };
            baseImg.onerror = reject;
            baseImg.src = uploadedImage;
        }));
    }
    
    Promise.all(drawPromises).then(() => {
      const drawRotated = (element: { x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number, rotation: number }, drawFn: () => void) => {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(element.x + element.width / 2, element.y + element.height / 2);
        ctx.rotate(element.rotation * Math.PI / 180);
        ctx.translate(-(element.x + element.width / 2), -(element.y + element.height / 2));
        drawFn();
        ctx.restore();
      };
      const elementDrawPromises: Promise<void>[] = [];
      if (processedImageElement) {
        const procImg = new window.Image();
        procImg.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
        elementDrawPromises.push(new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => { 
          procImg.onload = () => {
            drawRotated(processedImageElement, () => {
              ctx.drawImage(procImg, processedImageElement.x, processedImageElement.y, processedImageElement.width, processedImageElement.height);
            });
            resolve();
          };
          procImg.onerror = reject;
          procImg.src = processedImageElement.src;
        }));
      }
      imageElements.forEach(imgEl => {
        const genImg = new window.Image();
        genImg.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
        elementDrawPromises.push(new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
          genImg.onload = () => {
            drawRotated(imgEl, () => {
              ctx.drawImage(genImg, imgEl.x, imgEl.y, imgEl.width, imgEl.height);
            });
            resolve();
          };
          genImg.onerror = reject;
          genImg.src = imgEl.src;
        }));
      });
      return Promise.all(elementDrawPromises);
    }).then(() => {
      const drawRotated = (element: { x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number, rotation: number }, drawFn: () => void) => {
      
      shapeElements.forEach(shapeEl => {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(element.x + element.width / 2, element.y + element.height / 2);
        ctx.rotate(element.rotation * Math.PI / 180);
        ctx.translate(-(element.x + element.width / 2), -(element.y + element.height / 2));
        drawFn();
        ctx.translate(shapeEl.x + shapeEl.width / 2, shapeEl.y + shapeEl.height / 2);
        ctx.rotate(shapeEl.rotation * Math.PI / 180);
        ctx.translate(-(shapeEl.x + shapeEl.width / 2), -(shapeEl.y + shapeEl.height / 2));
        
        ctx.fillStyle = shapeEl.fillColor;
        ctx.fillRect(shapeEl.x, shapeEl.y, shapeEl.width, shapeEl.height);
        if (shapeEl.strokeWidth > 0) {
          ctx.strokeStyle = shapeEl.strokeColor;
          ctx.lineWidth = shapeEl.strokeWidth;
          ctx.strokeRect(shapeEl.x, shapeEl.y, shapeEl.width, shapeEl.height);
        }
        ctx.restore();
      };
      shapeElements.forEach(shapeEl => {
        drawRotated(shapeEl, () => {
          ctx.fillStyle = shapeEl.fillColor;
          if (shapeEl.type === 'rectangle') {
              ctx.fillRect(shapeEl.x, shapeEl.y, shapeEl.width, shapeEl.height);
          }
        });
      });
      textElements.forEach(textEl => {
        // For text, rotation center should ideally be its visual center.
        // Simple approximation: x, y is top-left.
        // The current rotation is around the div's top-left which matches the style.
        // To draw it correctly on canvas, we might need to adjust translate.
        // For now, we draw at textEl.x, textEl.y within the rotated context.
        const textCenterX = textEl.x + (ctx.measureText(textEl.content).width / 2); // Approximate
        const textCenterY = textEl.y + (textEl.fontSize / 2); // Approximate
        
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(textCenterX, textCenterY);
        ctx.translate(textEl.x + ctx.measureText(textEl.content).width / 2, textEl.y + textEl.fontSize / 2); // Approximate center
        ctx.rotate(textEl.rotation * Math.PI / 180);
        ctx.translate(-textCenterX, -textCenterY);
        ctx.translate(-(textEl.x + ctx.measureText(textEl.content).width / 2), -(textEl.y + textEl.fontSize / 2));
        
        ctx.fillStyle = textEl.color;
        ctx.font = `${textEl.fontSize}px sans-serif`; 
        ctx.textAlign = 'left';
        ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
        ctx.textAlign = 'left'; 
        ctx.textBaseline = 'top'; 
        ctx.fillText(textEl.content, textEl.x, textEl.y);
        ctx.restore();
      });
        
      const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
      link.download = `arttoo-design-${timestamp}.png`;
      link.href = dataUrl;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      toast({ title: "Design Downloaded", description: "Check your downloads folder." });
    }).catch(error => {
        console.error("Error during design download composition:", error);
        toast({variant: "destructive", title: "Download Failed", description: "Could not compose final image."});
    });
  };
  const handleCanvasClick = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (event.target !== canvasContainerRef.current) return; 
    if (activeTool === 'Text' && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      const rect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = event.clientX - rect.left;
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top;
      const defaultColor = typeof window !== "undefined" ? getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--foreground').trim() || 'hsl(0 0% 98%)' : 'hsl(0 0% 98%)';
      const newTextElement: TextElement = {
        id: Date.now().toString(),
        content: 'Your Text Here',
        x,
        y,
        fontSize: 16,
        color: defaultColor,
        rotation: 0,
      };
      setTextElements(prev => [...prev, newTextElement]);
      setSelectedTextElementId(newTextElement.id);
      setSelectedImageElementId(null);
      setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
      setActiveTool('Select');
    } else if (activeTool === 'Elements' && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      const rect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = event.clientX - rect.left - 50; 
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top - 25;
      const primaryColorHSL = typeof window !== "undefined" ? getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--primary-hsl')?.trim() || '257 57% 50%' : '257 57% 50%';
      const foregroundColor = typeof window !== "undefined" ? getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--foreground').trim() || 'hsl(240 10% 3.9%)' : 'hsl(240 10% 3.9%)';
      const newShapeElement: ShapeElement = {
        id: Date.now().toString(),
        type: 'rectangle',
        x: Math.max(0, x),
        y: Math.max(0, y),
        width: 100,
        height: 50,
        fillColor: `hsl(${primaryColorHSL} / 0.7)`,
        rotation: 0,
        strokeColor: foregroundColor,
        strokeWidth: 1,
      };
      setShapeElements(prev => [...prev, newShapeElement]);
      setSelectedShapeElementId(newShapeElement.id);
      setSelectedTextElementId(null);
      setSelectedImageElementId(null);
      setActiveTool('Select');
    } else if (activeTool === 'Select') {
      setSelectedTextElementId(null);
      setSelectedImageElementId(null);
      setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
    }
  };
  const handleTextElementMouseDown = (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && id === selectedTextElementId && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      setIsDraggingText(true);
      setDraggingTextElementId(id);
      const textEl = textElements.find(el => el.id === id);
      if (textEl) {
        const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        const mouseXInCanvas = event.clientX - canvasRect.left;
        const mouseYInCanvas = event.clientY - canvasRect.top;
        setDragStartOffsetText({ x: mouseXInCanvas - textEl.x, y: mouseYInCanvas - textEl.y });
      }
      document.body.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    }
  };
  
  const handleProcessedImageMouseDown = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => { 
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && processedImageElement && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target.classList.contains('cursor-nwse-resize')) {
        return;
        return; // Let resize handle this
      }
      
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      setIsDraggingProcessedImage(true);
      setSelectedTextElementId(null); 
      setSelectedImageElementId(null);
      setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
      const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
      const mouseXInCanvas = event.clientX - canvasRect.left;
      const mouseYInCanvas = event.clientY - canvasRect.top;
      setDragStartOffsetProcessedImage({ x: mouseXInCanvas - processedImageElement.x, y: mouseYInCanvas - processedImageElement.y });
      document.body.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    }
  };
  const handleGenericImageMouseDown = (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && canvasContainerRef.current) { 
      const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target.classList.contains('cursor-nwse-resize')) {
        return;
        return; // Let resize handle
      }
      setSelectedImageElementId(id);
      setSelectedTextElementId(null);
      setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
      
      // Initiate drag if it's the selected image or no image was previously selected (to select and drag in one go)
      if(id === selectedImageElementId || !selectedImageElementId) { 
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        setIsDraggingGenericImage(true);
        setDraggingGenericImageId(id);
        const imgEl = imageElements.find(el => el.id === id);
        if (imgEl) {
          const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
          const mouseXInCanvas = event.clientX - canvasRect.left;
          const mouseYInCanvas = event.clientY - canvasRect.top;
          setDragStartOffsetGenericImage({ x: mouseXInCanvas - imgEl.x, y: mouseYInCanvas - imgEl.y });
        }
        document.body.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
      }
    }
  };
  const handleShapeElementMouseDown = (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && canvasContainerRef.current) { 
        const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
        if (target.classList.contains('cursor-nwse-resize')) {
            return;
            return; // Let resize handle this
        }
        
        setSelectedShapeElementId(id);
        setSelectedTextElementId(null);
        setSelectedImageElementId(null);
        if(id === selectedShapeElementId || !selectedShapeElementId) { 
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setIsDraggingShapeElement(true);
            setDraggingShapeElementId(id);
            const shapeEl = shapeElements.find(el => el.id === id);
            if (shapeEl) {
                const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
                const mouseXInCanvas = event.clientX - canvasRect.left;
                const mouseYInCanvas = event.clientY - canvasRect.top;
                setDragStartOffsetShapeElement({ x: mouseXInCanvas - shapeEl.x, y: mouseYInCanvas - shapeEl.y });
            }
            document.body.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        }
    }
  };
  const handleShapeResizeHandleMouseDown = (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && id === selectedShapeElementId && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      const shapeEl = shapeElements.find(el => el.id === id);
      if (shapeEl) {
        setIsResizingShape(true);
        setResizeShapeStartPoint({ x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
        setResizeShapeStartDimensions({ width: shapeEl.width, height: shapeEl.height });
        // Ensure other drag/resize states are false
        setIsDraggingShapeElement(false); setIsDraggingText(false); setIsDraggingGenericImage(false); setIsDraggingProcessedImage(false); setIsResizingImage(false); setIsResizingProcessedImage(false);
        document.body.style.cursor = 'nwse-resize';
      }
    }
  };
  
  const handleImageResizeHandleMouseDown = (id: string, event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && id === selectedImageElementId && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      const imgEl = imageElements.find(el => el.id === id);
      if (imgEl) {
        setIsResizingImage(true);
        setResizingImageId(id);
        setResizeImageStartPoint({ x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
        setResizeImageStartDimensions({ width: imgEl.width, height: imgEl.height, naturalWidth: imgEl.naturalWidth, naturalHeight: imgEl.naturalHeight });
        // Ensure other drag/resize states are false
        setIsResizingShape(false); setIsDraggingShapeElement(false); setIsDraggingText(false); setIsDraggingGenericImage(false); setIsDraggingProcessedImage(false); setIsResizingProcessedImage(false);
        document.body.style.cursor = 'nwse-resize';
      }
    }
  };
  const handleProcessedImageResizeHandleMouseDown = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && processedImageElement && canvasContainerRef.current) {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      setIsResizingProcessedImage(true);
      setResizeProcessedImageStartPoint({ x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY });
      setResizeProcessedImageStartDimensions({ 
        width: processedImageElement.width, 
        height: processedImageElement.height, 
        naturalWidth: processedImageElement.naturalWidth, 
        naturalHeight: processedImageElement.naturalHeight 
      });
      // Ensure other drag/resize states are false
      setIsResizingShape(false); setIsDraggingShapeElement(false); setIsDraggingText(false); setIsDraggingGenericImage(false); setIsDraggingProcessedImage(false); setIsResizingImage(false);
      document.body.style.cursor = 'nwse-resize';
    }
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleGlobalMouseMove = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!canvasContainerRef.current) return;
      const canvasRect = canvasContainerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (isResizingShape && selectedShapeElementId && resizeShapeStartPoint && resizeShapeStartDimensions) {
        const deltaX = event.clientX - resizeShapeStartPoint.x;
        const deltaY = event.clientY - resizeShapeStartPoint.y;
        let newWidth = resizeShapeStartDimensions.width + deltaX;
        let newHeight = resizeShapeStartDimensions.height + deltaY;
        const MIN_DIM = 10;
        newWidth = Math.max(MIN_DIM, newWidth);
        newHeight = Math.max(MIN_DIM, newHeight);
        setShapeElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === selectedShapeElementId ? { ...el, width: newWidth, height: newHeight } : el));
      } else if (isResizingImage && resizingImageId && resizeImageStartPoint && resizeImageStartDimensions) {
        const deltaX = event.clientX - resizeImageStartPoint.x;
        let newProposedWidth = resizeImageStartDimensions.width + deltaX;
        
        const aspectRatio = resizeImageStartDimensions.naturalWidth / resizeImageStartDimensions.naturalHeight;
        let newWidth = Math.max(20, newProposedWidth);
        let newHeight = newWidth / aspectRatio;
        if (newHeight < 20) { 
            newHeight = 20;
            newWidth = newHeight * aspectRatio;
        }
        
        setImageElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === resizingImageId ? { ...el, width: newWidth, height: newHeight } : el));
      } else if (isResizingProcessedImage && processedImageElement && resizeProcessedImageStartPoint && resizeProcessedImageStartDimensions) {
        const deltaX = event.clientX - resizeProcessedImageStartPoint.x;
        let newProposedWidth = resizeProcessedImageStartDimensions.width + deltaX;
        
        const aspectRatio = resizeProcessedImageStartDimensions.naturalWidth / resizeProcessedImageStartDimensions.naturalHeight;
        let newWidth = Math.max(20, newProposedWidth);
        let newHeight = newWidth / aspectRatio;
        if (newHeight < 20) { 
            newHeight = 20;
            newWidth = newHeight * aspectRatio;
        }
        setProcessedImageElement(prev => prev ? { ...prev, width: newWidth, height: newHeight } : null);
      } else if (isDraggingText && draggingTextElementId) {
        const textEl = textElements.find(el => el.id === draggingTextElementId);
        if (!textEl) return;
        let newX = event.clientX - canvasRect.left - dragStartOffsetText.x;
        let newY = event.clientY - canvasRect.top - dragStartOffsetText.y;
        const estimatedTextWidth = textEl.content.length * (textEl.fontSize / 1.8); 
        const estimatedTextHeight = textEl.fontSize * 1.2;
        newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, canvasRect.width - estimatedTextWidth));
        newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, canvasRect.height - estimatedTextHeight));
        setTextElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === draggingTextElementId ? { ...el, x: newX, y: newY } : el));
      } else if (isDraggingProcessedImage && processedImageElement) {
        let newX = event.clientX - canvasRect.left - dragStartOffsetProcessedImage.x;
        let newY = event.clientY - canvasRect.top - dragStartOffsetProcessedImage.y;
        newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, canvasRect.width - processedImageElement.width));
        newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, canvasRect.height - processedImageElement.height));
        setProcessedImageElement(prev => prev ? { ...prev, x: newX, y: newY } : null);
      } else if (isDraggingGenericImage && draggingGenericImageId) {
        const imgEl = imageElements.find(el => el.id === draggingGenericImageId);
        if (!imgEl) return;
        let newX = event.clientX - canvasRect.left - dragStartOffsetGenericImage.x;
        let newY = event.clientY - canvasRect.top - dragStartOffsetGenericImage.y;
        newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, canvasRect.width - imgEl.width));
        newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, canvasRect.height - imgEl.height));
        setImageElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === draggingGenericImageId ? { ...el, x: newX, y: newY } : el));
      } else if (isDraggingShapeElement && draggingShapeElementId) {
        const shapeEl = shapeElements.find(el => el.id === draggingShapeElementId);
        if(!shapeEl) return;
        let newX = event.clientX - canvasRect.left - dragStartOffsetShapeElement.x;
        let newY = event.clientY - canvasRect.top - dragStartOffsetShapeElement.y;
        newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, canvasRect.width - shapeEl.width));
        newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, canvasRect.height - shapeEl.height));
        setShapeElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === draggingShapeElementId ? {...el, x: newX, y: newY} : el));
      }
    };
    const handleGlobalMouseUp = () => {
      if (isDraggingText || isDraggingProcessedImage || isDraggingGenericImage || isDraggingShapeElement || isResizingShape || isResizingImage || isResizingProcessedImage) {
        setIsDraggingText(false); setDraggingTextElementId(null);
        setIsDraggingProcessedImage(false);
        setIsDraggingGenericImage(false); setDraggingGenericImageId(null);
        setIsDraggingShapeElement(false); setDraggingShapeElementId(null);
        setIsResizingShape(false); setResizeShapeStartPoint(null); setResizeShapeStartDimensions(null);
        setIsResizingImage(false); setResizingImageId(null); setResizeImageStartPoint(null); setResizeImageStartDimensions(null);
        setIsResizingProcessedImage(false); setResizeProcessedImageStartPoint(null); setResizeProcessedImageStartDimensions(null);
        document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
      }
    };
    if (isDraggingText || isDraggingProcessedImage || isDraggingGenericImage || isDraggingShapeElement || isResizingShape || isResizingImage || isResizingProcessedImage) {
      window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleGlobalMouseMove);
      window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleGlobalMouseUp);
    }
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleGlobalMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleGlobalMouseUp);
      if (document.body.style.cursor !== 'default') {
        document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
      }
    };
  }, [
    isDraggingText, draggingTextElementId, dragStartOffsetText, 
    isDraggingProcessedImage, dragStartOffsetProcessedImage,
    isDraggingGenericImage, draggingGenericImageId, dragStartOffsetGenericImage, 
    isDraggingShapeElement, draggingShapeElementId, dragStartOffsetShapeElement, 
    isResizingShape, selectedShapeElementId, resizeShapeStartPoint, resizeShapeStartDimensions, 
    isResizingImage, resizingImageId, resizeImageStartPoint, resizeImageStartDimensions,
    isResizingProcessedImage, resizeProcessedImageStartPoint, resizeProcessedImageStartDimensions,
    shapeElements, textElements, imageElements, processedImageElement 
  ]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      if (activeTool === 'Uploads') {
        fileInputRef.current.onchange = handleElementImageUpload;
      } else {
        fileInputRef.current.onchange = handleBaseImageUpload;
      }
    }
  }, [activeTool, handleElementImageUpload, handleBaseImageUpload]);
  const handleUpdateTextContent = useCallback((id: string, newContent: string) => {
    setTextElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, content: newContent } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleUpdateTextFontSize = useCallback((id: string, newSize: number) => {
    setTextElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, fontSize: Math.max(8, newSize) } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleUpdateTextColor = useCallback((id: string, newColor: string) => {
    setTextElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, color: newColor } : el));
  }, []);
  
  const handleUpdateTextRotation = useCallback((id: string, newRotation: number) => {
    setTextElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, rotation: parseInt(newRotation.toString(), 10) } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleDeleteSelectedTextElement = useCallback(() => {
    if (selectedTextElementId) {
      setTextElements(prev => prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedTextElementId));
      setSelectedTextElementId(null);
      toast({ title: "Text Element Deleted" });
    }
  }, [selectedTextElementId, toast]);
  
  const handleDuplicateTextElement = useCallback(() => {
    if (selectedTextElementId) {
      const originalElement = textElements.find(el => el.id === selectedTextElementId);
      if (originalElement) {
        const newElement: TextElement = {
          ...originalElement,
          id: Date.now().toString(),
          x: originalElement.x + 10,
          y: originalElement.y + 10,
        };
        setTextElements(prev => [...prev, newElement]);
        setSelectedTextElementId(newElement.id);
        toast({ title: "Text Element Duplicated" });
      }
    }
  }, [selectedTextElementId, textElements, toast]);
  const handleUpdateProcessedImageRotation = useCallback((newRotation: number) => {
    setProcessedImageElement(prev => prev ? { ...prev, rotation: parseInt(newRotation.toString(), 10) } : null);
  }, []);
  const handleDeleteProcessedImageElement = useCallback(() => {
    setProcessedImageElement(null);
    toast({ title: "Image Overlay Deleted" });
  }, [toast]);
  const handleUpdateImageRotation = useCallback((id: string, newRotation: number) => {
    setImageElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, rotation: parseInt(newRotation.toString(), 10) } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleDeleteSelectedGenericImageElement = useCallback(() => {
    if (selectedImageElementId) {
      setImageElements(prev => prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedImageElementId));
      setSelectedImageElementId(null);
      toast({ title: "Image Element Deleted" });
    }
  }, [selectedImageElementId, toast]);
  const handleDuplicateImageElement = useCallback(() => {
    if (selectedImageElementId) {
      const originalElement = imageElements.find(el => el.id === selectedImageElementId);
      if (originalElement) {
        const newElement: ImageElement = {
          ...originalElement,
          id: Date.now().toString(),
          x: originalElement.x + 10,
          y: originalElement.y + 10,
        };
        setImageElements(prev => [...prev, newElement]);
        setSelectedImageElementId(newElement.id);
        toast({ title: "Image Element Duplicated" });
      }
    }
  }, [selectedImageElementId, imageElements, toast]);
  const handleUpdateShapeFillColor = useCallback((id: string, newColor: string) => {
    setShapeElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, fillColor: newColor } : el));
  }, []);
  
  const handleUpdateShapeRotation = useCallback((id: string, newRotation: number) => {
    setShapeElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, rotation: parseInt(newRotation.toString(), 10) } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleUpdateShapeStrokeColor = useCallback((id: string, newColor: string) => {
    setShapeElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, strokeColor: newColor } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleUpdateShapeStrokeWidth = useCallback((id: string, newWidth: number) => {
    setShapeElements(prev => prev.map(el => el.id === id ? { ...el, strokeWidth: Math.max(0, parseInt(newWidth.toString(), 10)) } : el));
  }, []);
  const handleDeleteSelectedShapeElement = useCallback(() => {
    if(selectedShapeElementId) {
      setShapeElements(prev => prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedShapeElementId));
      setSelectedShapeElementId(null);
      toast({ title: "Shape Element Deleted" });
    }
  }, [selectedShapeElementId, toast]);
  const handleDuplicateShapeElement = useCallback(() => {
    if (selectedShapeElementId) {
      const originalElement = shapeElements.find(el => el.id === selectedShapeElementId);
      if (originalElement) {
        const newElement: ShapeElement = {
          ...originalElement,
          id: Date.now().toString(),
          x: originalElement.x + 10,
          y: originalElement.y + 10,
        };
        setShapeElements(prev => [...prev, newElement]);
        setSelectedShapeElementId(newElement.id);
        toast({ title: "Shape Element Duplicated" });
      }
    }
  }, [selectedShapeElementId, shapeElements, toast]);
  const handleBringToFront = useCallback((elementType: 'text' | 'image' | 'shape') => {
    if (elementType === 'text' && selectedTextElementId) {
      setTextElements(prev => {
        const item = prev.find(el => el.id === selectedTextElementId);
        if (!item) return prev;
        return [...prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedTextElementId), item];
      });
    } else if (elementType === 'image' && selectedImageElementId) {
      setImageElements(prev => {
        const item = prev.find(el => el.id === selectedImageElementId);
        if (!item) return prev;
        return [...prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedImageElementId), item];
      });
    } else if (elementType === 'shape' && selectedShapeElementId) {
      setShapeElements(prev => {
        const item = prev.find(el => el.id === selectedShapeElementId);
        if (!item) return prev;
        return [...prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedShapeElementId), item];
      });
    }
    toast({ title: "Element Brought to Front" });
  }, [selectedTextElementId, selectedImageElementId, selectedShapeElementId, toast]);
  const handleSendToBack = useCallback((elementType: 'text' | 'image' | 'shape') => {
    if (elementType === 'text' && selectedTextElementId) {
      setTextElements(prev => {
        const item = prev.find(el => el.id === selectedTextElementId);
        if (!item) return prev;
        return [item, ...prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedTextElementId)];
      });
    } else if (elementType === 'image' && selectedImageElementId) {
      setImageElements(prev => {
        const item = prev.find(el => el.id === selectedImageElementId);
        if (!item) return prev;
        return [item, ...prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedImageElementId)];
      });
    } else if (elementType === 'shape' && selectedShapeElementId) {
      setShapeElements(prev => {
        const item = prev.find(el => el.id === selectedShapeElementId);
        if (!item) return prev;
        return [item, ...prev.filter(el => el.id !== selectedShapeElementId)];
      });
    }
    toast({ title: "Element Sent to Back" });
  }, [selectedTextElementId, selectedImageElementId, selectedShapeElementId, toast]);
  const isProcessedImageActiveForProperties = useMemo(() => 
    activeTool === 'Select' && 
    processedImageElement !== null && 
    selectedTextElementId === null && 
    selectedImageElementId === null && 
    selectedShapeElementId === null,
    [activeTool, processedImageElement, selectedTextElementId, selectedImageElementId, selectedShapeElementId]
  );
  const rightPropertiesItems = useMemo((): AccordionPanelItem[] => {
    const items: AccordionPanelItem[] = [];
    const isProcessedImageActiveForProps = activeTool === 'Select' && processedImageElement && !selectedTextElementId && !selectedImageElementId && !selectedShapeElementId;
    
    if (selectedTextElementId && activeTool === 'Select') {
      const selectedElement = textElements.find(el => el.id === selectedTextElementId);
      if (selectedElement) {
        items.push({
          value: "text-properties",
          title: "Text Properties",
          icon: Baseline,
          content: (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="text-content-area" className="text-xs font-medium">Content</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="text-content-area"
                  value={selectedElement.content}
                  onChange={(e) => handleUpdateTextContent(selectedTextElementId, e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Enter your text"
                  className="text-sm mt-1"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 items-end">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="text-font-size" className="text-xs font-medium">Font Size</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="text-font-size"
                    type="number"
                    value={selectedElement.fontSize}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateTextFontSize(selectedTextElementId, parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9"
                    min="8"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="text-color" className="text-xs font-medium">Color</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="text-color"
                    type="color"
                    value={selectedElement.color}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateTextColor(selectedTextElementId, e.target.value)}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9 w-full p-1"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="text-rotation" className="text-xs font-medium">Rotation (°)</Label>
                <Input
                  id="text-rotation"
                  type="number"
                  value={selectedElement.rotation}
                  onChange={(e) => handleUpdateTextRotation(selectedTextElementId, parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
                  className="text-sm mt-1 h-9"
                  step="1"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleSendToBack('text')}><ArrowDownToLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> To Back</Button>
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleBringToFront('text')}><ArrowUpFromLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> To Front</Button>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full"
                onClick={handleDuplicateTextElement}
              >
                <Copy className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Duplicate Text
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full border-destructive text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                onClick={handleDeleteSelectedTextElement}
              >
                <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Delete Text
              </Button>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground pt-1">ID: {selectedElement.id.substring(0,6)}...</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">X: {selectedElement.x.toFixed(0)}, Y: {selectedElement.y.toFixed(0)}</p>
            </div>
          ),
        });
      }
    } else if (isProcessedImageActiveForProps && processedImageElement) {
    } else if (isProcessedImageActiveForProperties && processedImageElement) {
        items.push({
            value: "processed-image-properties",
            title: "Image Overlay Properties",
            icon: LayersIconProp,
            content: (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div>
                        <Label htmlFor="proc-img-rotation" className="text-xs font-medium">Rotation (°)</Label>
                        <Input
                            id="proc-img-rotation"
                            type="number"
                            value={processedImageElement.rotation}
                            onChange={(e) => handleUpdateProcessedImageRotation(parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
                            className="text-sm mt-1 h-9"
                            step="1"
                        />
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs">X: {processedImageElement.x.toFixed(0)}, Y: {processedImageElement.y.toFixed(0)}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs">W: {processedImageElement.width.toFixed(0)}, H: {processedImageElement.height.toFixed(0)}</p>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      className="w-full border-destructive text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                      onClick={handleDeleteProcessedImageElement}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Delete Overlay
                    </Button>
                </div>
            )
        });
    } else if (selectedImageElementId && activeTool === 'Select') {
      const selectedElement = imageElements.find(el => el.id === selectedImageElementId);
      if (selectedElement) {
        items.push({
          value: "generic-image-properties",
          title: "Image Element Properties",
          icon: HardDriveUpload, 
          content: (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="gen-img-rotation" className="text-xs font-medium">Rotation (°)</Label>
                <Input
                    id="gen-img-rotation"
                    type="number"
                    value={selectedElement.rotation}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateImageRotation(selectedImageElementId, parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9"
                    step="1"
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs">ID: {selectedElement.id.substring(0,6)}...</p>
              <p className="text-xs">X: {selectedElement.x.toFixed(0)}, Y: {selectedElement.y.toFixed(0)}</p>
              <p className="text-xs">W: {selectedElement.width.toFixed(0)}, H: {selectedElement.height.toFixed(0)}</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleSendToBack('image')}><ArrowDownToLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> To Back</Button>
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleBringToFront('image')}><ArrowUpFromLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> To Front</Button>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full"
                onClick={handleDuplicateImageElement}
              >
                <Copy className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Duplicate Image
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full border-destructive text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                onClick={handleDeleteSelectedGenericImageElement}
              >
                <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Delete Image Element
              </Button>
            </div>
          ),
        });
      }
    } else if (selectedShapeElementId && activeTool === 'Select') {
      const selectedElement = shapeElements.find(el => el.id === selectedShapeElementId);
      if (selectedElement) {
        items.push({
          value: "shape-properties",
          title: "Shape Properties",
          icon: ShapesIconProp,
          content: (
             <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="shape-fill-color" className="text-xs font-medium">Fill Color</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="shape-fill-color"
                    type="color"
                    value={selectedElement.fillColor}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateShapeFillColor(selectedShapeElementId, e.target.value)}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9 w-full p-1"
                  />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 items-end">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="shape-stroke-color" className="text-xs font-medium">Stroke Color</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="shape-stroke-color"
                    type="color"
                    value={selectedElement.strokeColor}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateShapeStrokeColor(selectedShapeElementId, e.target.value)}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9 w-full p-1"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="shape-stroke-width" className="text-xs font-medium">Stroke (px)</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="shape-stroke-width"
                    type="number"
                    value={selectedElement.strokeWidth}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateShapeStrokeWidth(selectedShapeElementId, parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9"
                    min="0"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
               <div>
                <Label htmlFor="shape-rotation" className="text-xs font-medium">Rotation (°)</Label>
                <Input
                    id="shape-rotation"
                    type="number"
                    value={selectedElement.rotation}
                    onChange={(e) => handleUpdateShapeRotation(selectedShapeElementId, parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
                    className="text-sm mt-1 h-9"
                    step="1"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleSendToBack('shape')}><ArrowDownToLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> To Back</Button>
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handleBringToFront('shape')}><ArrowUpFromLine className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> To Front</Button>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full"
                onClick={handleDuplicateShapeElement}
              >
                <Copy className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Duplicate Shape
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="w-full border-destructive text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive"
                onClick={handleDeleteSelectedShapeElement}
              >
                <Trash2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Delete Shape
              </Button>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground pt-1">Type: {selectedElement.type} | ID: {selectedElement.id.substring(0,6)}...</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">X: {selectedElement.x.toFixed(0)}, Y: {selectedElement.y.toFixed(0)} | W: {selectedElement.width.toFixed(0)}, H: {selectedElement.height.toFixed(0)}</p>
            </div>
          )
        });
      }
    }
    
    
    if (items.length === 0) { 
        items.push(
          { value: "transform", title: "Transform", icon: MousePointerSquareDashed, content: "Select an element to see transform options." },
          { value: "adjustments", title: "Adjustments", icon: SlidersHorizontalIconProp, content: "Select an image or element for adjustments." },
          { value: "effects", title: "Effects", icon: SparklesIconProp, content: "Select an element to apply effects." },
          { value: "history", title: "History", icon: HistoryIconProp, content: "View and revert design changes." }
        );
    }
    return items;
  }, [
    selectedTextElementId, textElements, 
    activeTool, processedImageElement, 
    activeTool, processedImageElement, isProcessedImageActiveForProperties,
    selectedImageElementId, imageElements,
    selectedShapeElementId, shapeElements,
    handleUpdateTextContent, handleUpdateTextFontSize, handleUpdateTextColor, handleUpdateTextRotation,
    handleDeleteSelectedTextElement, handleDuplicateTextElement,
    handleUpdateProcessedImageRotation, handleDeleteProcessedImageElement, 
    handleUpdateImageRotation, handleDeleteSelectedGenericImageElement, handleDuplicateImageElement,
    handleUpdateShapeFillColor, handleUpdateShapeRotation, handleDeleteSelectedShapeElement, handleDuplicateShapeElement,
    handleUpdateShapeFillColor, handleUpdateShapeRotation, handleUpdateShapeStrokeColor, handleUpdateShapeStrokeWidth,
    handleDeleteSelectedShapeElement, handleDuplicateShapeElement,
    handleBringToFront, handleSendToBack
  ]);
  const defaultOpenAccordion = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedTextElementId) return "text-properties";
    if (selectedImageElementId) return "generic-image-properties";
    if (selectedShapeElementId) return "shape-properties";
    if (activeTool === 'Select' && processedImageElement && !selectedTextElementId && !selectedImageElementId && !selectedShapeElementId) return "processed-image-properties";
    if (isProcessedImageActiveForProperties) return "processed-image-properties";
    return "transform"; 
  }, [selectedTextElementId, selectedImageElementId, selectedShapeElementId, activeTool, processedImageElement]);
  }, [selectedTextElementId, selectedImageElementId, selectedShapeElementId, isProcessedImageActiveForProperties]);
  return (
    <SidebarProvider defaultOpen>
      <LeftNav activePageLabel="Design Studio" />
      <div className="flex flex-1">
        <SidebarInset className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          <TopBar />
          <div className="flex-1 flex overflow-hidden">
            <VerticalToolbar
              items={designStudioToolbarItems}
              activeToolLabel={activeTool}
              onToolItemClick={handleToolSelect}
            />
            <CanvasArea
              activeTool={activeTool}
              uploadedImage={uploadedImage}
              processedImageElement={processedImageElement}
              isProcessedImageActiveForProperties={isProcessedImageActiveForProperties}
              isProcessingBackground={isProcessingBackground}
              textElements={textElements}
              selectedTextElementId={selectedTextElementId}
              setSelectedTextElementId={setSelectedTextElementId}
              imageElements={imageElements}
              selectedImageElementId={selectedImageElementId}
              setSelectedImageElementId={setSelectedImageElementId}
              shapeElements={shapeElements}
              selectedShapeElementId={selectedShapeElementId}
              setSelectedShapeElementId={setSelectedShapeElementId}
              onImageUpload={handleBaseImageUpload} 
              onRemoveBackground={handleRemoveBackground}
              onDownloadDesign={handleDownloadDesign}
              onCanvasClick={handleCanvasClick}
              onTextElementMouseDown={handleTextElementMouseDown}
              onProcessedImageMouseDown={handleProcessedImageMouseDown}
              onGenericImageMouseDown={handleGenericImageMouseDown}
              onShapeElementMouseDown={handleShapeElementMouseDown}
              onShapeResizeHandleMouseDown={handleShapeResizeHandleMouseDown}
              onImageResizeHandleMouseDown={handleImageResizeHandleMouseDown}
              onProcessedImageResizeHandleMouseDown={handleProcessedImageResizeHandleMouseDown}
              fileInputRef={fileInputRef} 
              canvasContainerRef={canvasContainerRef}
            />
          </div>
        </SidebarInset>
        <RightProperties
          items={rightPropertiesItems}
          title="Design Properties"
          defaultOpenValue={defaultOpenAccordion}
        />
      </div>
    </SidebarProvider>
  );
}

Excellent! VICTORY INDEED! I'm glad we got that blank canvas sorted. Sometimes it's the seemingly simple things that can be tricky.

Now that the Design Studio reliably starts fresh, let's think about the next enhancement. We've got good basic manipulation for shapes (rectangles). A very common styling option for shapes, besides fill color, is their stroke (outline).

Chosen Next Step: Implement Stroke Color and Stroke Width for Rectangle Shapes

Reasoning:

Fundamental Styling: Stroke is a core visual attribute for shapes, allowing for outlines or border-only designs.
Complements Fill Color: Users will expect to control both the fill and the outline of shapes.
Extends RightProperties: We can easily add controls for stroke color and width to the existing "Shape Properties" panel.
Builds on Existing Structure: We'll update the ShapeElement interface and rendering logic.
Detailed Plan & Affected Files:

src/app/design-studio/page.tsx:
Update ShapeElement Interface:
Add strokeColor: string;
Add strokeWidth: number;
Initialization of New Shapes:
When a new rectangle is created in handleCanvasClick:
Set a default strokeColor (e.g., the current theme's foreground color: foregroundColor variable we already get for default text color).
Set a default strokeWidth (e.g., 1 or 2 pixels).
Rendering in CanvasArea:
Modify the styling of the rendered shape div.
The main div for the shape will now need to handle both fill and stroke.
The border style will be \${shapeEl.strokeWidth}px solid ${shapeEl.strokeWidth > 0 ? shapeEl.strokeColor : 'transparent'}``.
The backgroundColor will remain shapeEl.fillColor.
The selection outline (2px dashed hsl(var(--primary))) might need a slight outlineOffset if the stroke width is large, to prevent overlap. For instance, outlineOffset: Math.max(0, 2 - shapeEl.strokeWidth) + 'px'. This ensures the outline is visible just outside the stroke.
RightProperties Panel Enhancement:
In the "Shape Properties" accordion item content:
Add a Label and Input type="color" for "Stroke Color".
Add a Label and Input type="number" (min="0") for "Stroke Width (px)".
New Handler Functions (memoized with useCallback):
handleUpdateShapeStrokeColor(id: string, newColor: string): Updates the strokeColor for the specified shape.
handleUpdateShapeStrokeWidth(id: string, newWidth: number): Updates the strokeWidth (ensuring it's non-negative).
Duplication Logic: Ensure strokeColor and strokeWidth are copied when a shape is duplicated via handleDuplicateShapeElement.
Download Logic (handleDownloadDesign):
When drawing shapes onto the canvas for download:
After ctx.fillRect(), if element.strokeWidth > 0:
Set ctx.strokeStyle = element.strokeColor;
Set ctx.lineWidth = element.strokeWidth;
Call ctx.strokeRect(0, 0, element.width, element.height); (using the translated 0,0 coordinates for the shape).
This will give users much finer control over the appearance of their rectangle shapes.
